# Influora Production Audit: Release Candidate 115f698

**Date:** 2026-09-19
**Release candidate:** commit `115f698` (`115f698b9df0ca2735e9cb2f2c5fe656daf07b66`), branch `feat/meera-creator-phase-e`
**Final decision (Swapnil, CEO):** **NO-GO** at the original 2026-09-19 audit; **updated 2026-09-20 to CONDITIONAL GO-LIVE (invite-only beta)** once the six-lane P0 remediation wave below is accounted for — see the 2026-09-20 Remediation log entry and section 50 for the gating conditions. Overall evidence level **E3** (original), **E4** as of 2026-09-20. Overall confidence **75** (original), **90** as of 2026-09-20.
**Report author:** Tara (Reporting), from the Sage Team audit inputs in `audit-2026-09-19/_raw/`, with spot checks against the clean RC checkout.

How to read this report. Every significant claim cites an evidence ID. `EV-###` IDs come from the deduplicated register (`merged-register.json`). `XR-*` IDs are issues added by the cross-team reviewers. Severities, evidence scores (E0-E5) and confidences are the **final** values after adversarial verification (`verification-and-review.json`). Where the verifier changed a value, the register value is shown next to it. Findings the verifier REFUTED are shown as refuted and are not counted. No secret value appears anywhere in this report; committed credentials are described by location and shape only.

## Remediation log

**2026-09-19 — EV-001 (TrendPullJob boot failure)**
- **What changed:** `influora-api/src/main/java/com/influora/job/TrendPullJob.java` — added `@Autowired` (plus the `org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired` import and an EV-001-dated comment) to the production 5-arg constructor so Spring can resolve it unambiguously; the package-private 7-arg test-seam constructor is unchanged. No other line in the 516-line file changed. New regression test: `influora-api/src/test/java/com/influora/job/TrendPullJobWiringTest.java` (new, untracked), which registers `TrendPullJob` with `AnnotationConfigApplicationContext.register()` (real constructor-candidate resolution) rather than `registerBean(Class)`, so it genuinely fails without the fix.
- **Reviewers and verdicts:** Kavya Reddy (QA Lead) — **APPROVE_WITH_NOTES**; Priya (CTO) — **APPROVE_WITH_NOTES**. Both independently re-derived the fix's correctness by direct code reading (not by trusting the producer's report) and both independently re-scanned the codebase and confirmed that 3 sibling `TrendSourceClient` beans — `NewsApiTopHeadlinesClient`, `TmdbUpcomingClient`, `YouTubeMostPopularClient` (all `influora-api/src/main/java/com/influora/service/trendspark/ingest/`) — carry the identical unconditional-`@Component`-plus-two-untagged-constructors defect and will throw the same `BeanCreationException`/"No default constructor found" the instant `TrendPullJob`'s own resolution clears. Neither reviewer approved a claim that the backend boots.
- **Meera's test + boot evidence:** `mvn -B clean test` in the fix worktree: 3,679 run, 0 failures, 0 errors, 22 skipped, exit 0 (directly captured, not piped); `TrendPullJobWiringTest` 1/1 green. A real boot attempt (`spring-boot:run`, dev profile, throwaway MySQL `ev001_boot_20260919`, no Docker) was run both with and without the fix: **without** the fix, boot fails exactly as EV-001 describes (`BeanCreationException` creating `trendPullJob`, "No default constructor found"); **with** the fix, Spring resolves `TrendPullJob`'s constructor and the failure moves one bean deeper, to `newsApiTopHeadlinesClient`, with the identical "No default constructor found" error. The API still never reached "Started" and no HTTP port ever opened; the health/401/login/read-endpoint probes were not executed.
- **New evidence score / confidence:** E5 / 95 (Meera's verify pass; verdict recorded as `EV-001 FIXED - NEXT BLOCKER FOUND`).
- **Provenance:** The fix and its test exist **only** in the worktree `C:/Users/Sage world/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/fix-ev001`, checked out (detached) on top of commit `f076c0f`. Nothing has been committed to `main` or to this RC's branch, and the RC audited below (`115f698`) does not contain this fix. See EV-001's updated register and section-36 entries below for the exact status wording this implies.
- **New finding opened:** EV-175 — the 3 sibling-class boot blocker described above, registered P0 in sections 11 and 36, discovered by Meera's independent verify pass (not merely restated from the producer).

**2026-09-19 — EV-175 (TrendSourceClient sibling boot blockers)**
- **What changed:** The identical one-line `@Autowired` fix (plus the same import and an EV-175-dated comment) was added to the public production constructor of all 3 sibling classes — `influora-api/src/main/java/com/influora/service/trendspark/ingest/NewsApiTopHeadlinesClient.java`, `TmdbUpcomingClient.java`, `YouTubeMostPopularClient.java` — leaving each package-private test-seam constructor unannotated and untouched. New regression test: `influora-api/src/test/java/com/influora/service/trendspark/ingest/TrendSourceClientsWiringTest.java` (new, untracked), a 3-case parameterized `register()`-based wiring test mirroring `TrendPullJobWiringTest`, one case per sibling class.
- **Reviewers and verdicts:** Kavya Reddy (QA Lead) — **APPROVE**; Priya (CTO) — **APPROVE**. Both re-verified the fix by direct file read and each independently re-ran the per-class falsification (the single `@Autowired` line removed in binary mode, sha256-checked before/after, restored and sha256-matched). Priya additionally re-scanned all 344 `@Component`/`@Service`/`@Repository`/`@Controller`/`@RestController`/`@Configuration` classes in `src/main` for the same two-constructor/no-`@Autowired` shape and found 17 multi-constructor beans in total; confirmed all 17 (the 3 EV-175 classes, `TrendPullJob`, and 13 others) now carry `@Autowired` on exactly their production constructor and never on a test seam — no further undetected instance of this defect class exists in `src/main`.
- **Meera's test + boot evidence:** `mvn -B clean test` in the fix worktree, with both the EV-001 and EV-175 fixes applied: 3,682 run, 0 failures, 0 errors, 22 skipped, exit 0 (directly captured, not piped), BUILD SUCCESS, 3m08s — includes `TrendPullJobWiringTest` (1/1) and the new `TrendSourceClientsWiringTest` (3/3). A real `spring-boot:run` boot (dev profile, `SERVER_PORT=18080`, a fresh throwaway MySQL 8 database `ev001_boot_r1`, no Docker) **reached "Started InfluoraApiApplication"** on 3 separate runs (15.845s, and again for a probe run at 16.114s), with Flyway reporting "Successfully applied 137 migrations." No `BeanCreationException` and no "No default constructor found" appeared in any of these boots. Probes against the running instance: `GET /api/v1/health` → 200 `{"status":"UP"}` once the Mail and Redis health indicators were disabled (`MANAGEMENT_HEALTH_MAIL_ENABLED=false`/`MANAGEMENT_HEALTH_REDIS_ENABLED=false`); with default indicators on, the same endpoint reports 200 DOWN solely because `SMTP_HOST` is empty and Redis at `localhost:6379` is unreachable with Docker down — both pre-existing environment gaps, not defects in this fix. `GET /api/v1/config/public` → 200; `GET /api/v1/campaigns` and `GET /api/v1/wallet` with no token → 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED` as expected; `GET /api/v1/actuator/health` → 401 (pre-existing: `SecurityConfig.java:85` permits only `/health`, not `/actuator/**`, unrelated to this fix). The first 3 boot attempts hit an environment-only blocker (a JDK 21 Windows AF_UNIX selector-socket failure under the default `%TMP%` path), resolved by pointing `TMP`/`TEMP` at a short path plus `-Djdk.net.unixdomain.tmpdir`; this is host configuration, not a code defect, and did not recur once set.
- **New evidence score / confidence:** E5 / 97 (Meera's verify pass; three independent successful boots plus a clean, non-piped full-suite run).
- **Provenance:** The fix, its test, and every boot above exist **only** in the same worktree as the EV-001 fix, `C:/Users/Sage world/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/fix-ev001`, still detached on top of commit `f076c0f`. Nothing has been committed to `main` or to this RC's branch. Per the standing rule, this remains **NOT CONFIRMED FOR PRODUCTION until committed and re-verified from the commit** — see EV-175's and EV-001's updated register and §36 entries below.
- **Net effect:** With both fixes applied, the application reaches "Started" and serves requests for the first time anywhere in this audit — but only in an uncommitted worktree. No code on `main`, on this RC, or on any branch boots today. The overall decision remains **NO-GO**: the other P0s (money-integrity EV-002/EV-015/EV-005, the false regulatory claim EV-007, committed keys EV-006, EV-004/XR-PRIYA-01) are unaffected by this remediation and none of them have been addressed. *(Superseded by the commit-level entry immediately below — the fixes described here were subsequently committed and re-verified.)*

**2026-09-19 — EV-002 (escrow double funding — investigation, fix, and Meera-verified in a fix checkout)**
- **Investigation — is EV-002 live or dormant, and is the owner's webhook hypothesis correct?** Vikram and Kabir independently traced the production config and the live money path. Nothing in `application.yml`/`application-prod.yml` turns escrow off; the only `influora.escrow.*` key is `release-gate.cutover-instant`, unrelated to funding (`application.yml:146-155`). `LedgerEscrowBackend` is registered unconditionally as the sole `EscrowBackend` (`LedgerEscrowBackend.java:18-32`) — the only money-in-escrow path in prod is an internal ledger hold (brand wallet -> platform clearing wallet), not a regulated third-party escrow; that matches the team's standing decision to defer real Razorpay Route escrow until transaction volume justifies it (`project_payment_custody_licence_deferred.md`), but this internal "secured funds" hold is already live and moves real money. The only kill switch anywhere is the frontend flag `VITE_PAYMENTS_IN_ENABLED` (`true` in the committed `.env.production:65` and defaulted `true` in `publish-images.yml:186-187`), which gates the UI only and does not protect the API. **EV-002 is confirmed LIVE**, reachable from two real screens — the deal-room Payments tab (`deal-payments-tab.tsx:205`) and the brand-wallet Secure Funds card (`brand-wallet.tsx:1511`) — both calling `POST /wallet/escrow/fund` through `useEscrowFund`, whose Idempotency-Key lives in a per-mount `useRef` (`useEscrowFund.ts:148,178,203-208`) and is cleared by `reset()`, a reload, a second tab, or a remount. **The owner's webhook hypothesis is answered NO, with evidence.** `initiateFund` has never created a Razorpay order since commit `8deb097` (2026-07-27, "fix(escrow): stop double-charging on fund"), which removed the gateway leg entirely and funds straight from the wallet via `applyFunding(hold, null)`. That old commit does match the owner's memory of "happened once when the webhook was not there" — it was a gateway-plus-webhook double charge, and that leg is gone now. EV-002 today has no webhook or Razorpay step at all: a fresh `POST /wallet/escrow/fund` with a new Idempotency-Key for the same milestone passes every existing check and debits the brand wallet a second time purely through the synchronous `initiateFund` path (`EscrowService.java:261-274` dedupes only on the client key; `assertContractActiveForMilestone:388-434` never read `milestone.getStatus()`; `applyFunding:473-503` calls `milestone.markFunded(hold.getId())`, which overwrote `escrowHoldId` unconditionally — `PaymentMilestone.java:141-145`). Confirmed on a real MySQL 8 database (Meera's evidence below): the double funding reproduces with zero webhook involvement.
- **The fix.** `PaymentMilestone.markFunded` now throws `IllegalStateException` unless the milestone is `PENDING` with no hold already bound (`PaymentMilestone.java:141-160`). `EscrowService.assertContractActiveForMilestone` loads the milestone under a new `PESSIMISTIC_WRITE` lock (new `PaymentMilestoneRepository.findByIdAndWorkspaceIdForUpdate`) as its first read in the transaction, then a new `assertMilestoneFundable` check runs right after the Idempotency-Key replay and before any wallet-balance read or ledger post, refusing with `409 MILESTONE_ALREADY_FUNDED` for FUNDED/FROZEN/RELEASED/REFUNDED. A new `MILESTONE_CAMPAIGN_MISMATCH` check refuses a request whose body `campaignId` does not match the milestone's own collaboration. `applyFunding` (the legacy webhook path) got the same guard as defence in depth. Files: `EscrowService.java`, `PaymentMilestone.java`, `PaymentMilestoneRepository.java`, plus 3 new test files and edits to 5 existing test files (fixture statuses changed to `PENDING` so `markFunded` no longer throws in setup). Deliberately deferred: a DB-level `UNIQUE` index on `escrow_holds.milestone_id`, because prod may already hold a duplicate row from the incident the owner reported, which would fail the migration and block boot — see new finding EV-179 below. Deliberately left to the other session's uncommitted `F-0873` work: the campaign-level (no-milestone) pool-funding guard, per the standing "don't write a parallel guard" rule.
- **Reviewers and verdicts:** Kavya Reddy (QA Lead) — **APPROVE_WITH_NOTES**: independently reran the suite (87 targeted tests, 0 failures) in her own checkout, confirmed the 409-before-any-debit ordering by direct code read, and ran her own mutation (disabling the new gate) to confirm the tests actually catch the bug (5 of 13 failed, then restored and re-verified sha256-clean). Her required changes are process, not code: this diff conflicts with the other session's uncommitted `F-0873` in `EscrowService.java`/`EscrowServiceTest.java`/`EscrowHoldRepository.java`, so it must land *after* F-0873, with the EV-002 milestone-fundable check kept first and one F-0873 test stub updated (`findByIdAndWorkspaceId` -> `findByIdAndWorkspaceIdForUpdate`); run the prod duplicate-row check before the index migration; route a 409-handling and deterministic-idempotency-key follow-up to Ananya/Arjun on the frontend. Priya (CTO) — **APPROVE_WITH_NOTES**: independently confirmed `initiateFund` is genuinely `@Transactional` and not self-invoked, traced lock ordering across `ContractService`/`DealService`/`CampaignService`/`WalletLedgerService` and found no reverse-lock path, and confirmed via `wiki/decisions/influora-launch-payments-2026-07-25.md` that the code this fix touches (the internal ledger hold) **is** the flow already live for real transactions today, not the deferred Razorpay Route — so the fix is in scope regardless of the escrow-later decision. Same required changes as Kavya, plus documenting the lock-ordering rule in `wiki/tech/architecture.md`.
- **Meera's runtime evidence — verdict PASS.** `mvn -B clean test` in the fix checkout: BUILD SUCCESS, 3752 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 22 skipped (Docker-gated only); the new `EscrowServiceMilestoneDoubleFundTest` (13/13), `PaymentMilestoneMarkFundedTest` (6/6) and `PaymentMilestoneRepositoryForUpdateTest` (3/3) all passed. **A real-MySQL concurrency probe directly answers the owner's question:** at baseline `fb6d267` (no fix), two threads funding the same milestone with different Idempotency-Keys both succeeded in every round tested — 2 `escrow_holds` rows, 2 brand-wallet debit legs, the milestone's `escrow_hold_id` left pointing at only one hold (the other orphaned), and the wallet balance and ledger rows disagreeing after the race — **and no webhook was involved anywhere in this reproduction.** With the fix applied, the same race produced exactly 1 hold, 1 debit, and the losing thread got a clean `409 MILESTONE_ALREADY_FUNDED` with zero new rows; a third fresh-key attempt on the now-funded milestone also got `409` with zero new rows. A `spring-boot:run` boot from the fix checkout reached "Started InfluoraApiApplication" and served `GET /api/v1/health` -> 200 UP. Evidence score/confidence: **E5 / 90**. Meera also flagged a pre-existing, unrelated defect present at HEAD and unchanged by this fix — a roughly 50-second stall plus a silently-dropped `FUND_ESCROW` history event on every milestone funding call, caused by `ApplicationHistoryService`'s `REQUIRES_NEW` insert deadlocking against the `initiateFund` collaboration-row lock — flagged to Arjun/Vikram as a separate ticket, out of EV-002's scope and not assigned an EV number here (this remediation log records new findings from Kabir/Vikram only, per the task scope).
- **New findings from Kabir/Vikram, added to the register (section 11) as EV-176 through EV-180:** EV-176 (P1) campaign-level pool funding has no server-side state guard; EV-177 (P1) the uncommitted F-0873 dedupe can be bypassed with a mismatched `campaignId`; EV-178 (P2) F-0873's dedupe reads a stale snapshot under MySQL REPEATABLE READ; EV-179 (P1) prod may already hold a duplicate `escrow_holds.milestone_id` row, blocking any unique-index migration; EV-180 (P3) a lagging Razorpay top-up webhook can make a brand pay an escrow-funding shortfall twice (overpayment, not a double debit — both payments are credited once each).
- **Status change:** **EV-002 is now FIXED — VERIFIED IN FIX CHECKOUT, NOT YET COMMITTED (NOT CONFIRMED FOR PRODUCTION UNTIL COMMITTED AND RE-VERIFIED FROM THE COMMIT).** See EV-002's updated register entry (section 11) and section 36 entry below for the exact status wording this implies. This does not change the overall decision: it remains **NO-GO**, because EV-004, EV-005, EV-006, EV-007, EV-008, EV-015 and XR-PRIYA-01 remain open; `fb6d267` (the EV-001/EV-175 fix) is committed locally only and not pushed; and this EV-002 fix additionally still needs to be merged onto the other session's uncommitted `F-0873` work in the main tree before it can be committed at all.

**2026-09-19 — EV-002/EV-176/EV-177/EV-178 (committed and verified from the commit, de249ed)**
- **What changed:** The EV-002 fix described in the entry above was committed to `feat/meera-creator-phase-e` as commit `de249ed` (`fix(escrow): one hold per milestone and per campaign pool - stop double funding (EV-002, EV-176, EV-177, EV-178, F-0873)`), parent `fb6d267`. The commit is **not pushed** to any remote. The repo owner chose to merge the EV-002 fix onto the other session's previously-uncommitted `F-0873` campaign-level pool-funding work rather than defer it, so `de249ed` lands both the milestone-level EV-002 guard and a campaign-level pool guard together; the main-tree working copies of the touched files were synced to the committed versions and no work was lost. `git show --stat de249ed` lists exactly **13 files, 1545 insertions, 24 deletions**: 4 main-source files (`PaymentMilestone.java`, `EscrowHoldRepository.java`, `PaymentMilestoneRepository.java`, `EscrowService.java`) and 9 test files (`PaymentMilestoneMarkFundedTest`, `EscrowHoldRepositoryCampaignLevelForUpdateTest`, `PaymentMilestoneRepositoryForUpdateTest`, `DisputeEscrowConcurrencyTest`, `EscrowServiceIdempotencyTest`, `EscrowServiceMilestoneDoubleFundTest`, `EscrowServiceReleaseOutcomeTest`, `EscrowServiceReleaseTest`, `EscrowServiceTest`).
- **Reviewers and verdicts (pre-merge):** Kavya Reddy (QA Lead) and Priya (CTO) — both **APPROVE_WITH_NOTES** on the pre-merge diff (see the EV-002 entry above for the substance); their required process condition — merge onto `F-0873` before commit — is what this commit does. Pre-merge suite: `mvn -B clean test` — **3763** run, **0** failures, **0** errors, **22** skipped (Docker-gated only, unchanged skip set).
- **Meera's commit-level concurrency evidence:** 7 independent real-MySQL races against `de249ed` (a mix of milestone-level and campaign-pool-level) never produced 2 holds or a 2nd debit — every race resolved to exactly 1 hold/1 debit with a clean 409 for the loser or a later fresh-key retry. A baseline race run against the parent commit `fb6d267` (pre-EV-002-fix) reproduced the double debit in the same harness, with **no webhook involved anywhere in the path** — confirming the defect is real at the commit boundary and that the fix, not a change in the harness, closes it.
- **Webhook hypothesis — reconfirmed at the commit level:** the historic incident the brand owner remembers ("happened once when the webhook wasn't there") matches a different, already-fixed incident — the gateway-plus-webhook double charge closed by commit `8deb097` (2026-07-27). EV-002 itself has never involved a webhook or a Razorpay order since that commit; it is a purely synchronous double-debit through `initiateFund`, which the commit-level baseline race above reproduces with zero webhook involvement.
- **Status change:** **EV-002, EV-176 and EV-177 are now CLOSED — FIXED IN COMMITTED CODE de249ed, VERIFIED FROM THE COMMIT.** **EV-178 is FIXED IN de249ed** (the dedupe read is now the same locking read used for the EV-002 guard, not a follow-up plain SELECT), **but stays OPEN as a P3 follow-up**: a real-MySQL REPEATABLE-READ snapshot proof via a Testcontainers integration test is still owed before it can be fully closed. See the updated register rows (section 11) and the EV-002 entry in section 36 below.
- **New finding EV-181 (P1, pre-existing, not introduced by this fix; E4/85):** every milestone fund now stalls for roughly `innodb_lock_wait_timeout` (~50s default) and silently loses its `FUND_ESCROW` application-history row. `ApplicationHistoryService.record` runs in `REQUIRES_NEW` (`ApplicationHistoryService.java:69`) and its insert (`application_history_events.application_id` FK -> `collaborations`) waits on the same collaboration row the outer `initiateFund` transaction holds `FOR UPDATE` (`EscrowService.java` ~:525-527); a genuinely concurrent duplicate attempt then gets a raw MySQL 1205 lock-wait-timeout (likely surfacing as a 5xx) instead of the new clean 409. Observed by Meera on real MySQL: 0 history rows after 4 funds, with "Could not write the application-history event" in the log. This stall plus a frontend timeout-and-retry is flagged as a plausible trigger for past duplicate-fund reports, possibly including the incident the owner referenced. **Recommended fix:** write the history event after commit (a `@TransactionalEventListener(phase = AFTER_COMMIT)` listener) or fold it into the same transaction instead of `REQUIRES_NEW`; map the resulting `PessimisticLockingFailureException` to a 409/retryable response instead of a 5xx. Added to the register (section 11) and to section 37 (P1 Findings) below.
- **Residual follow-ups (P3 unless noted):** a possible InnoDB gap-lock deadlock between the first pool-funds of two different campaigns racing each other (money-safe — InnoDB rolls one transaction back, no double debit); confirm via `EXPLAIN` that the new locking query uses `idx_escrow_campaign`; a replayed campaign-pool fund request returns the existing hold even if the requested amount differs from the original request (product may prefer a 409 instead of a silent replay); frontend follow-up for Ananya/Arjun — treat `409 MILESTONE_ALREADY_FUNDED` as refresh-to-funded rather than a bare error, and make `useEscrowFund`'s idempotency key deterministic per milestone; **EV-179 remains open, unchanged** — the read-only prod duplicate-hold check must still run before any unique-index migration.
- **Overall decision:** unchanged, **NO-GO**. EV-004, EV-005, EV-006, EV-007, EV-008 and EV-015 remain open P0s; `de249ed` and its parent `fb6d267` are committed locally only, not pushed, and no new RC has been cut from either.

**2026-09-20 — EV-004, EV-005, EV-006 (code part), EV-007, EV-031, XR-ROHAN-01, EV-008, EV-015 (six-lane P0 wave, committed and verified from the commits)**

See the recomputed Executive Summary, sections 36/37 and the readiness matrix below for the full basis.

## 1. Executive Summary

**Decision: NO-GO for RC 115f698 itself — nothing from that release candidate may be deployed to production, and the currently-live build keeps serving.** **Updated 2026-09-20:** on top of RC 115f698's own branch, `feat/meera-creator-phase-e` now carries nine committed, commit-verified P0 fixes (EV-001, EV-175, EV-002 on 2026-09-19; EV-004, EV-005, EV-006 code part, EV-007, EV-008, EV-015 on 2026-09-20), none of them pushed and none deployed. With those nine accounted for, the only P0 left open in the register is XR-PRIYA-01, so **the decision for a future RC cut from this branch moves to CONDITIONAL GO-LIVE (invite-only beta)**, gated on credential rotation, pushing and re-verifying from a new RC, a real Docker/CI Testcontainers run, legal sign-off on the remaining EV-007 items, and closing or scoping out the findings listed as still-open below. See the 2026-09-20 Remediation log entry and section 50.

**Why, in order of weight:**

1. **The backend cannot start on the audited RC 115f698 (EV-001, P0, E5/C98) or on any other prior commit. Status: CLOSED — FIXED IN COMMITTED CODE fb6d267, VERIFIED FROM THE COMMIT (suite + real boot + probes).** `TrendPullJob` is an unconditional `@Component` with two constructors, neither annotated `@Autowired` (`TrendPullJob.java:156` and `:175`). Spring fails context refresh with `BeanCreationException ... No default constructor found`. This was reproduced twice on the RC: meera-run booted the RC in the default and dev profiles, and the verifier ran a standalone bean-factory probe against the RC's own bytecode and pinned Spring version. Deploying RC 115f698 would have taken down every brand, creator, admin, payment, webhook and Meera path. The defect was introduced on 2026-09-18, one day before the RC, and no test caught it because the only full-context tests need Docker and were skipped (XR-PRIYA-02, EV-038). **2026-09-19 update:** a one-line `@Autowired` fix plus a new wiring test were built, tested and boot-verified in an uncommitted fix worktree (see Remediation log above), and both Kavya and Priya reviewed and approved it; the identical fix for 3 sibling `TrendSourceClient` beans (new finding EV-175, P0) was then built and verified the same way. **2026-09-19, commit `fb6d267` (final update):** both fixes were committed on `feat/meera-creator-phase-e` (parent `7846d93`, not pushed) and re-verified from a clean checkout of that commit — Kavya independently re-reviewed and APPROVEd the diff, and Meera's commit-level verify pass got a full `mvn clean test` (3,730/0/0, 22 skipped) plus a real `spring-boot:run` boot that reached "Started InfluoraApiApplication" and served `/api/v1/health` (200 UP), `/api/v1/config/public` (200), and 401 on `/api/v1/campaigns`/`/api/v1/wallet` — see the third Remediation log entry above. EV-001 and EV-175 are both now CLOSED. **This no longer makes the decision NO-GO by itself** — but the decision stays **NO-GO** because EV-002, EV-004, EV-005, EV-006, EV-007, EV-008 and EV-015 remain open, and `fb6d267` is committed locally only, not pushed and not deployed.
2. **The money core is not safe for real money, even once boot is fixed — as of the original audit.** Verification confirmed four defects:
   - a milestone that is already FUNDED or RELEASED can be funded again, debiting the brand twice (EV-002, P0) — **CLOSED 2026-09-19, committed `de249ed`, verified from the commit**;
   - a brand OWNER/ADMIN can refund a funded hold to itself after the creator's work is approved, and this applies to every milestone (EV-015, upgraded to P0) — **CLOSED 2026-09-20, committed `a76ea77`, verified from the commit**;
   - `POST /campaigns` with `status=ACTIVE` skips both the funded-escrow check and the publish fee (EV-005, P0) — **CLOSED 2026-09-20, committed `9bb8055`, verified from the commit** (the ledger invariant for the publish fee still needs the Docker/CI-only `CampaignActivationLedgerIntegrationTest`, unproven locally);
   - any workspace member, VIEWER included, can approve a deliverable and so trigger a release (EV-021, P1). This is hidden today only because approval never actually releases anything (EV-019). **Both remain OPEN**, untouched by any remediation pass.

   Money-out is also broken and **remains OPEN**. Every creator withdrawal fails at commit (EV-014). The withdrawal calls the gateway inside an open transaction (EV-003). Affiliate settlement cannot post at all (EV-011, EV-025, EV-033).
3. **The live site made a false regulatory claim (EV-007, P0) — CLOSED for its copy part 2026-09-20, committed `51577f5`, verified from the commit.** Customer-facing copy said an RBI-authorized Payment Aggregator holds brand funds and that Influora "never pools" them, while in the code funds move through an Influora-owned pooled clearing wallet. The regulated-custody, pooling and guarantee language was removed and a new `ev007-regulated-claims.test.ts` gate added (5/5 green). Two further claims closed the same way: "Guaranteed payment" (XR-ROHAN-01) and TDS on automated payouts (EV-031, copy only — TDS still is not computed on the automated path). **Legal items remain OPEN**: counsel review of who legally holds wallet balances/insolvency treatment, TDS applicability, the RBI-KYC citation still in the privacy policy, and the stale OG image.
4. **Live-format provider keys were committed in tracked `env.example` files and in git history (EV-006, P0) — CLOSED for its code/placeholder part 2026-09-20, committed `6228d61`, verified from the commit** (`ci/secret-scan.py` now green, plus a new CI secret-scan workflow). **Rotation remains OPEN and is a hard prerequisite before this branch is ever pushed**: the commit's own diff shows the real values on the removed side of the hunks, and all six credentials (Anthropic, Sarvam, both R2 keys, both MSG91 keys) are still valid and still sit in `origin`'s history.
5. **Other P0s — both CLOSED 2026-09-20:**
   - A percent-encoded path got past the `/internal/**` service-token filter (EV-004) — **CLOSED, committed `be76665`, verified from the commit** with live probes (percent-encoded, double-encoded and case-variant paths all 401 with a real brand JWT that gets 200 on ordinary routes). XR-PRIYA-01 (the same bug class already fixed in a sibling filter, plus the missing shared-utility/architecture-test gap) is **not** in this wave's close list and **stays OPEN**, even though `be76665`'s new `RequestPaths.java` may bear on it.
   - Creator follower counts that creators typed in themselves were shown as Meta-verified (EV-008) — **CLOSED, committed `c6ed523`, verified from the commit** on the public profile endpoint (probed live: a 900,000 self-declared count renders with `followersSource IMPORTED`/`isVerified false`, no field labels it verified). The same commit's changes to admin creator stats, brand discovery/search and the Meera `show_creators` tool are unit-tested but were not probed live in this pass.

**Scale of the result.** 240 raw findings were deduplicated into 174 register entries. Cross-review added 5 more. Final severities at the original 2026-09-19 audit were **9 P0** (8 EVs plus XR-PRIYA-01), **21 P1**, **77 P2** and **70 P3**. One finding was refuted (EV-034) and one was excluded as uncommitted and unresolved (EV-026). The verifier re-examined the 36 highest-severity findings. **The same-day EV-001 remediation pass (2026-09-19) added one further register entry, EV-175 (P0, boot blocked by 3 sibling classes), bringing the P0 count to 10 as of the worktree-only stage. With EV-001, EV-175 and EV-002 CLOSED (2026-09-19) and, now, EV-004, EV-005, EV-006 (code part), EV-007, EV-008 and EV-015 also CLOSED (2026-09-20, six commits on `feat/meera-creator-phase-e`, all committed and verified from their commits, none pushed), the current open P0 count is 1: XR-PRIYA-01.** Of the P1s, EV-031 and XR-ROHAN-01 are also CLOSED as of 2026-09-20 (copy fixed in `51577f5`); EV-021 and the other money-out/affiliate P1s remain open, unaffected by this wave.

## 2. Audit Scope

**In scope:**
- **The release candidate.** Commit `115f698`, audited from a clean checkout at `C:/Users/Sage world/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/rc-115f698` (git status clean, re-confirmed at the end of the runtime pass). It covers three services:
  - the Vite/React SPA (114 routes);
  - the Spring Boot 3.3.5 monolith `influora-api` (91 controllers, 342-343 endpoint mappings, 87 entities over 89 tables, 24 scheduled job units);
  - the FastAPI `influora-ai` service (10 routes).

  Deploy and CI configuration were in scope: Dockerfiles, 4 committed compose topologies, 10 GitHub workflows and nginx/Caddy configs.
- **The working-tree delta.** The uncommitted changes on top of the RC were audited separately (delta pass, section 7) so that "what ships next" is not confused with "what the RC contains".
- **The live site, read-only.** `influora.in` was probed with GET requests, browser loads and TLS observation only (Neha, Aditya, Priya). There were no logins, form submissions or state changes.

**Out of scope or not reachable:**
- Live production environment values: the VPS `.env`, GitHub Actions variables and run history, the RazorpayX account, and the untracked live compose file `/usr/local/App/docker-compose.prod.yml`.
- Live provider calls: Anthropic, Gemini, Sarvam, Meta, Razorpay.
- Authenticated live flows.
- Search Console data.
- Load testing.
- Docker-based integration tests. Docker was unavailable, so all 22 Testcontainers tests skipped.

**Rules applied:**
- Evidence comes only from the RC checkout plus the listed runtime runs.
- Code comments and docs count as context, not proof.
- No secrets are printed.
- No invented customers, revenue, traction, market size or AI accuracy.

## 3. Methodology

The audit ran as a pipeline. Every stage wrote JSON to `_raw/`.

1. **Inventory** (`inventory.json`). A regex census of controllers, endpoints, routes, AI routes and scheduled jobs, plus 11 domain groupings and the frontend caller for each endpoint.
2. **Specialist lanes** (21 agents, one JSON each). The lanes were:
   - architecture (Priya);
   - security (Kabir);
   - QA and anti-vibe-code (Kavya);
   - database (Meera-DB);
   - runtime build/test/boot (Meera-Run);
   - API (Vikram-API);
   - payments (Vikram-Pay);
   - frontend (Ananya);
   - performance (perf);
   - AI (Ash);
   - automation and integrations (Dev);
   - live end-to-end (Neha);
   - SEO (Aditya);
   - content ops (Nisha);
   - copy (Ishaan);
   - design and contrast (Zara);
   - costs (Rohan);
   - product and business (Swapnil);
   - marketing claims (Tejas);
   - entity and job wiring (msa);
   - the working-tree delta (Kavya second pass).
3. **Domain blueprints** (11 agents). Each traced every feature in its domain end to end: entry point, API, database, state machine, authN/authZ, tests, working and failure conditions. Together they cover 97 features.
4. **Team questions.** Agents raised 473 sharp questions, and most were answered inline by the asking agent. Questions left open, or ones that crossed lanes, were routed through q-pipeline, q-tools and q-answerer. The answers are in `q-answers.json` (30 answers).
5. **Dedup and register** (Arjun). The 240 raw findings were merged into 174 register entries, `EV-001` to `EV-174`, with 39 recorded merges and 16 root-cause clusters. Several E5 claims were re-checked against code.
6. **Adversarial verification.** Skeptic verifiers re-examined the 36 highest-severity entries against the RC and issued CONFIRMED, CONFIRMED-RUNTIME, UPGRADED, DOWNGRADED, UNRESOLVED or REFUTED verdicts. Two of them ran runtime probes: a bean-factory probe for EV-001, and executed regression tests that refuted EV-034.
7. **Cross-team review.** Four reviewers challenged the register: Priya (architecture), Kabir (security), Kavya (QA) and Rohan with Tejas (finance and marketing). They recorded agreements and disagreements and added 5 missed issues (`XR-*`).
8. **CEO decision** (`final-decision.json`). Swapnil's decision, the 21-area readiness matrix, the must-fix order, the can-wait list, and the unresolved disagreements, which are recorded rather than averaged.
9. **This report.** Tara assembled the report and spot-checked specifics against the RC. Spot checks confirmed:
   - `TrendPullJob` constructors at `:156`/`:175` with no `@Autowired`;
   - `InternalServiceTokenFilter` reads the raw `getRequestURI()`;
   - `requireFundedEscrow`/`chargeOnPublish` are called only from `update()` in `CampaignService`;
   - no migration adds `AFFILIATE_*` values to the `wallet_transactions` ENUMs.

**Tooling note.** The project mandates graphify, but `graphify-out/graph.json` does not exist in the RC checkout (EV-105). Every agent therefore fell back to direct Read/Grep and executed commands, and each agent recorded that it had done so.

## 5. Evidence & Confidence Methodology

**Evidence score (E).** How strong the proof is, independent of how confident the author is.

| E | Meaning as applied in this audit |
|---|---|
| E5 | Multiple independent sources: code plus runtime, code plus executed test, or two agents independently reproducing. Single-agent E5 claims were capped at E4 unless they had both code and runtime proof (Arjun rule). |
| E4 | Direct code trace plus one executed confirmation (test run, runtime probe, live GET), or a strong code trace re-confirmed by a second reader. |
| E3 | Direct code or config trace with file:line citations; nothing executed. |
| E2 | Partial trace, inference from structure, or a single un-rechecked reading. zara findings carried no score and were defaulted to E2/C50. |
| E1 | Absence-based or analogy-based claim ("nothing found that does X"). |
| E0 | Nothing found; the question's premise could not be located. |

Tara's summary of E0-E4 is inferred from how scores were assigned across the inputs. The E5 definition and the E5 cap are quoted from the register and reviewer rubric.

**Confidence (0-100).** The author's probability that the claim is right as stated. Reviewers noted that static-only findings should rarely exceed about 85.

**Status labels.**

| Label | Meaning |
|---|---|
| VERIFIED | Re-checked by a second party or executed |
| CONFIRMED (independent finders) | Two or more agents found it separately |
| OBSERVED | Directly seen in code or config by one agent |
| INFERRED | Reasoned from structure |
| POSITIVE/INFORMATIONAL | A control that works, recorded to prevent regressions |

**Provenance labels.**

| Label | Meaning |
|---|---|
| COMMITTED | In the RC |
| UNCOMMITTED | Only in the working tree |
| CONFIGURATION | Depends on env or deploy values |
| UNKNOWN | Live state not observable |

**Verification verdicts** (applied to the 36 register entries rated P0 or P1, EV-001 to EV-036):
- **CONFIRMED-RUNTIME**: re-proved by execution.
- **CONFIRMED**: re-proved by independent code reading.
- **UPGRADED / DOWNGRADED**: the final severity changed.
- **UNRESOLVED**: out of RC scope.
- **REFUTED**: the defect is not present. It is dropped and shown only as refuted.

**Two confidences, never averaged.** Confidence that NO-GO is correct is about 97, because it rests on EV-001 alone. Confidence that this register is a complete and correctly-weighted picture of readiness is 75, at evidence level 3. The limits on the second number are:
1. Because of EV-001 no request was ever served, so there is no end-to-end runtime proof of any money, auth or API flow.
2. The 22 Testcontainers suites skipped, and real GitHub Actions history was never pulled.
3. Several green money tests mock the exact layer where the bug lives (EV-011, EV-014, EV-025).
4. Live env values were not inspected.
5. The main tree diverges from the RC under concurrent sessions.
6. Coverage was sampled and uneven across agents.
7. Nine disagreements remain unresolved (section 34).

## 7. Committed vs Uncommitted Analysis

The RC and the tree that would ship next are **not the same thing** (EV-018, root cause RC-10). The working tree carries one large, internally consistent, uncommitted feature bundle. It passes its own targeted tests, but it was caught not compiling mid-audit because of concurrent edits.

| Change | What it does | Where it lives | Relationship to RC findings |
|---|---|---|---|
| F-0848 | Single campaign-activation choke point (`CampaignActivationGuard`) plus a two-step brand publish UI | Uncommitted | Would address EV-005, the create() to ACTIVE bypass (root cause RC-04). Not in the RC, so EV-005 stands. |
| F-0872 | `Campaign.setStatus` structurally refuses ACTIVE | Uncommitted | Same activation-hardening family as EV-005 and EV-074 |
| F-0873 | Escrow duplicate-fund-attempt dedup | Uncommitted | Partial answer to EV-002. Introduces EV-098: the guard ignores a FROZEN (disputed) hold. |
| F-0857 / F-0858 | Delta-charge of the brand publish fee on resume | Uncommitted | Addresses EV-080 (a PAUSED resume collides at 409). Introduces EV-026: a second fee debit with no GST invoice update, self-disclosed. EV-026 is UNRESOLVED and excluded from RC scoring. |
| F-0964 | Public verified metrics only from Meta-synced rows | Committed after the RC (`1f1bf9f`) | EV-008 fix, not re-verified |
| F-0961 | Analytics headline, growth and trend read only Meta-synced rows | Committed after the RC (`e67dc5b`) | EV-008 fix, not re-verified |
| F-0589 | Portfolio on-time rate nullable (never a false 100%) | Uncommitted | Honesty fix on public portfolio data |
| F-0588 | N+1 fix in portfolio stats | Uncommitted | Addresses EV-072 |
| F-0965 | `applyAggregatedStats` renamed to `applyFollowerTotals` with a `followers_source` column | Uncommitted; appeared mid-audit from a concurrent session | Caused the transient compile failure |
| Proof-os gate state | The `citations` gate flipped from pass (2026-09-04) to fail (2026-09-17) | Working tree `.proof-os/rc/citations.rc` | EV-164 (P3) |

**Implications:**
1. The next RC's contents are currently undefined. Freeze main, then either commit or shelve F-0848 as an explicit decision (a must-fix launch condition).
2. The fixes for EV-005 and EV-008 exist, but the first is uncommitted and the second is committed but unverified. Neither can be credited to this RC.
3. EV-026 and EV-098 must be re-audited when their code is committed.
4. Per the verifier, EV-018 is a confirmed P1. Rohan and Tejas record that it is main-tree scope, not an RC defect. Both positions are kept (section 34).

## 9. Feature Working Conditions

For each domain feature, the conditions under which the blueprint agent found it works, the conditions under which it fails or is blocked, and the production prerequisites. The text is condensed from `blueprint-*.json`; `[...]` marks a cut at a sentence boundary. EV-001 is a precondition for every backend row.


**Auth and accounts**

| Feature | Works when | Fails / blocked when | Production conditions |
|---|---|---|---|
| Brand email-OTP signup/verify/register/login | A previously-unregistered email can send+verify an OTP (if requireEmailOtpBeforeRegister is on) or skip straight to register (default off), get a 201 with a stripped TokenPair plus an HttpOnly refresh cookie, and subsequently log in with the same credentials, landing in the newly created workspace. | Duplicate email/phone -> 409 EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS/PHONE_ALREADY_EXISTS (upfront check + DataIntegrityViolationException catch for the TOCTOU race, AuthService.java:127-163,193-221). Weak password -> PasswordPolicy exception. Missing/malformed phone -> 400 PHONE_REQUIRED / validation error. [...] | influora.auth.require-email-otp-before-register (default false) and influora.auth.require-email-verification (default true) must be set consistently, or a signup can be bricked at 2nd login per the F-0601 history [...] |
| Creator email-OTP signup/verify/register/login | An email can complete OTP (if required), register as a creator (optionally via an invite token), and log in; a suspended creator_profiles row blocks login even though users.status is ACTIVE. | Same 409/400/401/403 codes as brand, plus a creator-specific 403 ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED sourced from CreatorProfile.isSuspended() rather than User.status (AuthService.java:503-512). | Same env flags as brand flow. Invite-token linking depends on RegistrationService and external_creators being populated by the admin creator-connect flow (out of this domain's direct scope). |
| Token refresh + logout | A valid, unrevoked, unexpired refresh token (cookie or body) yields a new access token and a rotated refresh cookie; the account/workspace suspension gates are re-evaluated on every call, not just at login. | Missing token (cookie AND body) -> 401 INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN (AuthController.java:140-143). Revoked/expired/unknown hash -> 401 INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN, and the presented token is NOT burned in that case (guards run before the revoke/rotate block, AuthService.java:556-568 comment). [...] | JwtService's access/refresh expiry configuration and AuthCookieService's cookie flags (HttpOnly/Secure/SameSite) must be correctly set per environment; [...] |
| Forgot/reset password | forgot-password always returns the same generic message regardless of whether the email exists (anti-enumeration); reset-password with a valid unexpired unused token for a non-deleted, non-suspended user rotates the password hash, marks email verified, and revokes every outstanding session. | Invalid/expired/used token -> 400 INVALID_RESET_TOKEN. Soft-deleted user (deletedAt != null) -> same 400 INVALID_RESET_TOKEN, deliberately not a distinct code per AuthService.java:701-721's reasoning (already proven mailbox ownership means no enumeration risk). [...] | influora.web-base-url must be set (used to build the reset link) and PasswordResetEvent's listener/mailer must actually be wired for non-dev profiles to deliver the email at all (out of this domain's direct file scope [...] |
| Account self-service: DELETE /me/account, POST /me/password, GET/PATCH /users/me | An authenticated user can delete their own account (immediately losing all sessions), change their password (keeping their own current session alive per the e60d249 fix), and read/patch their own first/last/display name via PATCH (no password field accepted there, per UserDtos.UpdateProfileRequest having no password property -- [...] | deleteAccount: USER_NOT_FOUND 404 if the row is somehow already gone. changePassword: wrong currentPassword -> 401 INVALID_CURRENT_PASSWORD; SUSPENDED/DEACTIVATED users.status -> 403 (same never-written-in-product-code caveat as resetPassword); weak newPassword -> PasswordPolicy exception. | Depends on BrandContextService/CreatorContextService actually being wired into every brand/creator-scoped controller for the deletedAt check to have the claimed immediate effect -- this pass verified the claim only via [...] |
| Admin login/refresh/logout/me + TOTP MFA setup/verify | A correct password (+ correct TOTP code if mfa_enabled, or outright login-refusal via MFA_ENROLLMENT_REQUIRED if the role requires MFA but it is unenrolled) issues an access+refresh token pair; /me returns the caller's own AdminUserDto; MFA setup/verify enroll a new TOTP secret two-step (stage then confirm). | Bad password/unknown email -> generic 401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS (enumeration-safe, Kabir P1-2 Gap 3). Locked out (either counter) -> same generic 401, no distinguishing signal. Inactive admin -> 403 ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED. MFA required but code missing -> 401 MFA_REQUIRED. [...] | AdminSecurityProperties.lockoutMaxAttempts/lockoutCooldownSeconds/mfaEnforceOnLogin/mfaLockoutMaxAttempts/mfaLockoutCooldownSeconds must all be set sensibly per environment; [...] |
| Admin MFA reset POST /admin/auth/mfa/reset/{id} | An active, MFA-satisfied SUPER_ADMIN can clear a DIFFERENT active admin's MFA enrollment and immediately kill that target's outstanding sessions, forcing re-enrollment. | Non-SUPER_ADMIN caller -> rejected by AdminContextService before any row is touched (test: nonSuperAdminCallerIsRejected). Self-targeting -> 403 CANNOT_RESET_OWN_MFA (test: selfResetIsRejected). Inactive/nonexistent target -> ADMIN_NOT_FOUND via loadActive. | No FE caller exists for this endpoint (per the task's own pointer and not contradicted by anything found in src/admin in this pass) -- recovery today would have to be performed via a direct API call (curl/Postman) by an [...] |
| JWKS GET /.well-known/jwks.json, GET /config/public, GET /health | JWKS returns exactly the public key(s) with a kid, serializable without the documented Jackson/jjwt RedactedSupplier crash; /config/public's requireEmailOtp flag matches AuthService's own enforcement; /health returns DOWN (not silently 'ok') when the DB is unreachable. | None modeled beyond infra failure (DB down -> /health reports DOWN via actuator). | influora.auth.require-email-otp-before-register must be set identically to the value AuthService itself reads, since they are two independent @Value bindings of the same property key with no shared config object -- a [...] |
| SecurityConfig permitAll list; dead matcher GET /auth/verify-email | The permitAll list exactly matches the set of endpoints that are genuinely meant to be reachable pre-authentication, with no matcher that either (a) exposes something it shouldn't or (b) is silently dead because no backing endpoint exists. | A permitAll matcher with no backing endpoint is harmless (it can never be hit) but is a maintenance/audit hazard: it makes the security policy harder to reason about and could mislead a future reviewer into thinking a GET /auth/verify-email endpoint exists. | N/A. |
| Jobs: StaleTokenCleanupJob, IdempotencyReservationReaperJob | Both jobs run on their cron schedule, take a ShedLock to prevent double-execution across instances, single-flight-guard against re-entrancy within one instance (AtomicBoolean running), and per-row try/catch so one bad row never aborts the batch. | A per-row exception is caught, logged, and counted as failed/skipped without aborting the rest of the sweep (both jobs share this pattern explicitly). | Requires shedlock's distributed-lock table to be present and correctly migrated for multi-instance deployments; not independently re-verified in this pass. |

**Brand workspace and team**

| Feature | Works when | Fails / blocked when | Production conditions |
|---|---|---|---|
| Brand onboarding — company / KYC / complete / status / kyc-prompt-dismissed | A brand-new signup can complete steps 1-3, workspace row is created/updated with a unique slug, user.onboardingCompleted flips true, and GET /onboarding/brand/status reflects it on the very next request from any device/session. | Duplicate slug -> SLUG_NOT_AVAILABLE 409 (WorkspaceSlugService, untested — see finding); non-owner/admin write -> 403 via requireRole; missing user row -> USER_NOT_FOUND 404 | R2StorageService must be available/configured for the (currently dead) KYC-doc presign path to do anything if ever wired up; influora.web-base-url and R2Properties.publicUrl must be set. |
| Workspace profile — slug-check / me / mine / PATCH me | PATCH /workspaces/me full-replaces name/email/phone/industry/companySize/websiteUrl/description/logoUrl and the same values read back exactly via a subsequent GET /workspaces/me, including previously-omitted fields (see F-0462/F-0468 field-preservation comments). | Blank name -> VALIDATION_ERROR 400; bad email/phone format -> VALIDATION_ERROR 400; non-member -> requireMember throws; insufficient role -> requireRole throws 403 | None beyond the standard DB connection. |
| Team members — invite / accept / remove / list / invites / revoke / switch | An OWNER/ADMIN invites an email; the invitee (after logging in with that exact email) redeems the token via /accept, gets a workspace_members row, and can then use /switch to move their session into that workspace and back to their own. | Seat cap reached -> UPGRADE_REQUIRED 402 (active+pending accounting); duplicate pending invite -> resent, not duplicated; invite email mismatch -> 403 INVITE_EMAIL_MISMATCH; expired/revoked/already-accepted invite -> 410/409; deactivating the sole active OWNER -> 409 CANNOT_REMOVE_SOLE_OWNER; [...] | influora.web-base-url must be set for invite links to resolve correctly; Msg91EmailClient must be configured for a not-yet-registered invitee's email to actually send (dev mode short-circuits to a log line instead). |
| Brand dashboard actions + pipeline | actions() returns one ActionItem per REVIEW_PENDING collaboration and one per FUNDED milestone, each with a deep link and a computed deadline (REVIEW_SLA_DAYS=3 from collaboration.createdAt, or milestone.dueDate/updatedAt); pipeline() returns only stages with count > 0, matching the Pipeline page's own stage vocabulary. | Empty workspace -> honest empty lists (documented as deliberate, not fabricated placeholders); a collaboration referencing a deleted campaign falls back to the literal string "Campaign" rather than erroring | None beyond standard DB connectivity. |
| FE pages — /brand/settings, /brand/settings/verification, /brand/help, /brand/how-it-works | brand-settings.tsx loads and saves workspace fields without clobbering fields the form doesn't surface (F-0462/F-0468); brand-verification.tsx renders the correct one of {loading, verified, pending, not-permitted, form} state and recovers from a role-query rejection via a Retry affordance (F-0279); [...] | GET /workspaces/me rejection on settings load -> disables Save, never seeds mock values (verified by test); role-query rejection on verification page -> shows a retry banner but still renders the KYC form (fails open, server re-checks permission independently) | None beyond standard API availability. |

**Campaigns and discovery**

| Feature | Works when | Fails / blocked when | Production conditions |
|---|---|---|---|
| Campaign CRUD/duplicate/analytics (/campaigns*) | A verified brand member with the right role can list/create/update/delete/duplicate campaigns scoped to their own workspace and pause/resume/complete/cancel via a status-only PATCH; analytics returns creator-reported deliverable metrics via DeliverableMetricService. | Unverified workspace blocks ACTIVE (WORKSPACE_NOT_VERIFIED, 403); missing store integration blocks DIRECT campaigns (NO_STORE_INTEGRATION, 409); PATCH to ACTIVE without a FUNDED EscrowHold is rejected (ESCROW_NOT_FUNDED, 409) — but CREATE with status=ACTIVE is NOT rejected (CAMP-01, P0). | MySQL campaigns/collaborations/escrow_holds tables; no special env vars beyond normal DB config. |
| HYPE campaign flow (FE /brand/campaigns/new/hype, App.tsx:269) | A brand fills the Hype form and clicks Launch; budget is server-derived as rate x slots (a hand-crafted mismatched budget is unrepresentable, not merely rejected — CampaignService.java:481-513); a partial Meera-drafted HYPE campaign cannot reach ACTIVE until perReelRate/slotCap are set. | Malformed/partial hype config 400s (VALIDATION_ERROR); a partial draft cannot publish (testPartialHypeDraftCannotBePublished). | Same DB as Campaign CRUD. |
| Campaign templates (/campaign-templates*), plan-gated | list returns 4 SYSTEM + the caller's own CUSTOM templates; saveAsTemplate 402s a Free-plan workspace and 201s a Pro one; delete 400s on SYSTEM, 403 cross-workspace, and succeeds for the owning workspace's own CUSTOM template. | Cross-workspace GET of a CUSTOM template 404s (requireVisible, existence not leaked); cross-workspace DELETE 403s instead (documented intentional asymmetry per a Kavya QA note). | SYSTEM templates depend on the V20260714150000 migration having run. |
| Campaign report export GET /campaigns/{id}/export | A Pro-plan brand member downloads a CSV or PDF of a campaign's exact on-screen analytics numbers, filename 'campaign-{id}-report.{format}' (no PII in the filename by design). | Non-Pro workspace 402s (UPGRADE_REQUIRED); unknown format value 400s (INVALID_FORMAT); a foreign campaign 404s (CAMPAIGN_NOT_FOUND). | PDF rendering depends on ReportPdfService (not opened in this pass). |
| Creator discovery list/search/featured/suggestions/similar/get/profile/save/invite (/creators/*) | search/featured/suggestions/similar return batched (no N+1 per-creator query) creator data with real quality/authenticity/brand-safety scores where scored (never fabricated 0s — null when unscored) and a real average star rating (H-22 fix, never the old algorithmic quality score mislabeled as a rating); [...] | Inviting an already-invited (non-CANCELLED) creator 409s (COLLABORATION_EXISTS); inviting to a DRAFT campaign 409s (CAMPAIGN_NOT_PUBLISHED); saving past the plan's saved-creator cap 402s (UPGRADE_REQUIRED). | Standard MySQL; SubscriptionService.getActivePlanForWorkspace resolves the saved-creator cap. |
| External creators list/lookup/connect/connection-requests | list degrades cleanly to the table-only view when Marketplace/Meta calls fail or are unconfigured (never fabricates a card — explicit class-level design principle); lookup resolves Business Discovery live and upserts, correctly handling IG username renames and concurrent-insert races via REQUIRES_NEW-isolated recovery re-reads; [...] | lookup 503s when Meta isn't configured/connected (INSTAGRAM_LOOKUP_UNAVAILABLE) or 404s when the IG account doesn't exist (CREATOR_NOT_FOUND); connect 429s over the daily cap (CREATOR_CONNECT_RATE_LIMITED); an already-JOINED creator 409s via CreatorAlreadyOnInfluoraException. | Requires MetaApiProperties configuration and a usable brand/system/creator FACEBOOK_LOGIN token; degrades gracefully (never fails hard) when absent. |
| Creator marketplace browse/detail/apply | A creator browses ACTIVE, non-private, deadline-open campaigns (with in-memory niche/platform post-filtering scanning up to 1,000 rows, POST_FILTER_SCAN_LIMIT), views one, and applies exactly once, producing both a visible deal-room timeline entry and a durable, AFTER_COMMIT-safe application-history event even for the [...] | CAMPAIGN_NOT_OPEN, APPLICATION_DEADLINE_PASSED, ALREADY_APPLIED, CAMPAIGN_NOT_FOUND (draft or unauthorized-private). | No special production conditions. |
| Creator applications + history (/creator/applications*) | Lists the creator's own applications (never brand invites) sorted by appliedAt desc, with an optional status filter and page/limit pagination (MAX_LIMIT=50); returns the full ordered history timeline for one owned application. | Unknown status filter value 400s (INVALID_STATUS); a foreign or nonexistent dealId on history 404s (DEAL_NOT_FOUND). | No special production conditions. |
| Job: ScoreCalculationJob (0 0 4 UTC) | Runs daily at 04:00 UTC, scores every discoverable creator that has at least one metrics snapshot, gracefully skips (logs, does not error) creators with no metrics yet, and optionally brand-safety-scores a capped, staleness-prioritized rotating subset when the feature flag is on. | Per-creator exceptions are caught and logged, never abort the batch (mirrors MetricsPollingJob's resilience pattern); a brand-safety-scoring failure degrades only the 3 brand-safety columns to null on that row, never the fake-follower/quality/rate scores on the same row. | Requires the ShedLock table/mechanism to be configured for the distributed lock to work; BrandSafetyScoringProperties.isEnabled() is OFF by default (documented Priya ruling — cost/volume concern), so the 3 brand-safety [...] |

| Deal lifecycle: list / get / create / accept / reject / counter | A brand with OWNER/ADMIN/MANAGER membership can propose a deal within campaign budget/collaborator-cap limits; either party can counter/accept/reject while status is pre-contract; [...] | 409 DEAL_NOT_REJECTABLE once CONTRACT_PENDING+; 409 COLLABORATION_EXISTS on duplicate propose (DB unique constraint backstop); budget/collaborator-cap/agreed-rate validation errors on createProposal/counter; concurrent reject/accept resolved via row lock + idempotency replay, not silent overwrite | Standard DB + JWT auth; no external service dependency for this flow itself |
| Deal-room messaging incl. SSE stream | Both roles can list/send/mark-read messages on their own deal; the stream authorizes first, sends a comment-only heartbeat frame to open cleanly, and reconnects with backoff+jitter on ANY disconnection (including a clean server close, per the CR-31 fix) rather than going silently deaf | A gap longer than the server's replay-buffer retention window is unrecoverable via Last-Event-ID and falls back to onReconnect triggering a full refetch; unauthorized dealId returns 403/404 before any emitter exists | Depends on the reverse proxy (Caddy, per comments) not buffering the SSE response; this was not independently verified against the actual deployed proxy config in this source-only pass |
| Shipments: address / mark-shipped / confirm-receipt / get | Creator submits/updates an address before shipping; brand marks shipped with carrier/tracking (trackingUrl is scheme-validated to http/https only); creator confirms receipt in good or damaged condition; GET returns a synthetic AWAITING_ADDRESS response when no row exists yet rather than 404 | 409 SHIPMENT_ALREADY_SHIPPED on address edit after shipping; 409 SHIPMENT_NO_ADDRESS if brand tries to ship with no address on file; 409 SHIPMENT_NOT_SHIPPED if creator confirms receipt before SHIPPED; [...] | Depends on notification system (ShipmentCreatedEvent/ShipmentReceivedEvent) and on CreatorContextService/BrandContextService resolving correctly; no Docker/Testcontainers dependency beyond the standard DB |
| Contracts: create / list / unsigned / get / sign / pdf-download-url | Brand generates a contract with milestones; each party signs with their own typed full name (signerName, F-0292); once both signatures are recorded the contract auto-advances to ACTIVE and a PDF is generated; pdf-download-url 404s with CONTRACT_PDF_NOT_READY until both signed and PDF generation completed | A genuinely NEW signature takes a PESSIMISTIC_WRITE lock on the Collaboration row and re-checks CANCELLED under that lock (CR-22a, closing a race against a concurrent DealService#doReject) — but an idempotent replay of an already-recorded signature deliberately skips this to avoid touching the [...] | Requires the PDF-generation pipeline and presigned-URL storage (R2/S3-shaped) to be configured; contract PDF readiness gating depends on that pipeline completing before pdf-download-url returns 200 |
| Contract cancel / amend (API-only, no FE caller) | A brand or creator can cancel a DRAFT/PENDING_SIGNATURES contract; a brand can amend an ACTIVE contract into a new version | 409 CONTRACT_NOT_CANCELLABLE outside the allowed states | None beyond standard DB |
| Brand deliverable review: get / approve / revise / reject / safety-review | Approve on a SUBMITTED/RESUBMITTED deliverable with a funded, unfrozen, unreleased milestone releases escrow and returns success; approve with no/unfunded/already-released/frozen/refunded milestones still succeeds but surfaces the correct reason instead of a false success; [...] | 409 on invalid state transitions (testApproveInvalidState/testReviseInvalidState/testRejectInvalidState); revision-limit-reached rejection; escrow-release-fails-unexpectedly rolls back cleanly (testApproveRollsBackWhenEscrowReleaseFailsUnexpectedly) with a dedicated isolation test [...] | Escrow release depends on EscrowService/wallet configuration; safety-review depends on whatever AI/content-moderation backend DeliverableSafetyReviewService calls (not traced in this pass) |
| Creator deliverables: list / bulk / upload / status / submit / metrics / proof / mark-posted / verify | Creator uploads real files (MIME-sniffed, not just extension-trusted — testUploadMimeSpoofRejected), submits for review (deadline-aware but never auto-penalizing late submission per an explicit F-0418 ruling), reports metrics from POSTED/APPROVED states, marks posted with a URL that passes a strict per-platform validator, and [...] | 409 on invalid-state transitions for every mutating action; markPosted rejects http (non-https), file:, javascript: schemes, localhost/loopback/private-range/cloud-metadata IPs, lookalike/path-spoofed hosts, and overlong/XSS-tail URLs — each with its own dedicated test | File upload depends on R2/object storage configuration; verify depends on the Meta Graph API integration (DeliverableVerificationService) being configured with valid credentials |
| Disputes: raise / list / eligible-deals | Either party opens a dispute only against a deal with real, unreleased, funded escrow; brand and creator each see a display-enriched (campaign/counterparty name) list of their own disputes; a creator sees a filtered eligible-deals dropdown excluding DISPUTED/COMPLETED/CANCELLED collaborations and non-funded ones | 409 NO_FUNDED_ESCROW, 409 DISPUTE_ALREADY_OPEN on openDispute | Depends on EscrowService's freeze/hold primitives being correctly configured |
| Two-way reviews: submit / received / flag | A party can leave exactly one review after COMPLETED, read reviews received about them, and flag a received review once with a non-blank reason | 409 COLLABORATION_NOT_COMPLETED, 409 ALREADY_REVIEWED (both pre-check and DB-race paths), 400 INVALID_REQUEST on blank flag reason, 409 ALREADY_FLAGGED, 404 REVIEW_NOT_FOUND when the review doesn't belong to the caller's own collaboration (IDOR-safe by returning NOT_FOUND rather than FORBIDDEN, [...] | None beyond standard DB |
| Jobs: DeliverableVerificationJob, DeliverableCleanupJob (x2 sweeps) | DeliverableVerificationJob runs at most once per 6h window (ShedLock lockAtMostFor PT2H, lockAtLeastFor PT1M, plus a per-instance AtomicBoolean re-entrancy guard) and tallies outcomes without a single deliverable's failure aborting the sweep | A per-deliverable exception is caught and logged, not propagated; ShedLock prevents two instances or two overlapping triggers from double-running | Requires ShedLock's backing table/lock provider to be configured for multi-instance deployments; DeliverableVerificationJob depends on the Meta Graph API integration being reachable and credentialed |

## 10. Feature Blueprints (condensed)

The full per-feature blueprints (flow, database, state machine, authN, security conditions, required fixes and evidence) are in the companion `FEATURE_BLUEPRINT.html`. The same data is in `_raw/blueprint-*.json` if the HTML is not present. This section gives, per domain, each feature's entry point or API, its authorization model, its test coverage as found, and its known issues.


#### 10.1 Auth and accounts
