## 12. Frontend

**Status: PARTIAL.** It builds, but there are live defects on the money and support surfaces. **E4 / C80** (CEO matrix).

**Executed evidence (meera-run, Ananya, Kavya):**
- `tsc --noEmit` exits 0 and runs as its own CI gate (EV-118).
- `npx vite build` exits 0: 4,905 modules, main chunk 3,373.53 kB (899.29 kB gzip), plus an 894 kB `PerformanceMonitor` chunk.
- `vitest`: 1,348 passed, 2 failed, 3 skipped across 241 files. One failure is a load flake that passes in isolation. The other, `creator-protected-route.test.tsx`, deterministically times out importing `@/App`, so the F-0459 remember-me storage-split guard never executes (EV-055).
- `eslint`: 7 errors and 219 warnings. The warnings follow the documented react-hooks v7 warn policy. The errors are real `no-unused-vars` (EV-119). No CI workflow runs lint at all (XR-KAVYA-01).
- The payments-gate suite passes 9/9 and asserts that `fetch` is never called for withdraw, fund or top-up when money flags are off (EV-120).

**Defects:**
- **Meera funding.** `/brand/meera` pays against the hardcoded id `meera_demo_campaign`, which gets a 404 from `deriveFundAmount`. Its "Confirm payment" link goes to `/brand/escrow/fund`, which has no SPA route (EV-024, P1).
- **Support.** `/support` is a "coming soon" placeholder even though the requester support-ticket API is built and tested (EV-012, P1).
- **CTA contrast.** Hype-solid primary CTAs render white text at 2.43:1 in light mode and 1.81:1 in dark mode (EV-032, P1). Four stage badges and the Meera escrow green also fail AA (EV-102, EV-103).
- **Admin session.** A dead admin session only clears `localStorage` and never forces re-login, and the admin token is persisted in `localStorage` (EV-029, downgraded to P2).
- **Bundle.** Zero route-level code splitting (EV-039).
- **Soft 404.** Unknown single-segment URLs soft-404 with an empty "@" handle message (EV-042, runtime-reproduced live).
- **Built but never rendered:** `useMeeraCredits`/`CreditMeter` (EV-065), `CounterProposalCard` (EV-133), `HeroGlobe` (EV-137).
- **Mock fixtures.** A mock "Kavala Skincare" theme renders on the live `/brand/meera` (EV-043).
- **FE/BE type drift.** `fundEscrow` declares a non-nullable `razorpayOrderId` that the backend always sends as null (EV-154).

**Positives:**
- Brand and creator sessions keep the access token in memory with an HttpOnly refresh cookie, and use a hardened 401, refresh, retry, redirect path (Ananya).
- The Razorpay checkout wrapper never trusts the client handler as proof of payment.
- 0 `console.log` and 0 `@ts-ignore` (Kavya).
- The main client has zero stale endpoint calls (EV-131).

**Uncertainty:** Visual and interactive states of the roughly 100 pages were sampled, not exhaustively exercised. The live bundle (`index-CuJLpqhw.js`) does not match a same-day RC build (`index-VeUi4oUB.js`), so the live frontend is an older, unidentified build (EV-010, Neha).

## 13. Backend

**Status: NOT READY. It cannot boot.** **E5 / C95.**

**Executed:**
- `mvn -B clean test` ran 3,621 tests: 0 failures, 0 errors, 22 skipped. All 22 skips are Testcontainers suites across 8 classes, including `EscrowReleaseGateIntegrationTest` (7) and `DatabaseConstraintIntegrationTest` (3).
- `spring-boot:run` in the default profile fails closed on the placeholder GSTIN (`CompanyTaxStartupValidator`). That is correct behaviour, and it means prod must supply a real GSTIN.
- In the dev profile, Flyway, JPA and the whole security filter chain initialise, and then `trendPullJob` fails instantiation (EV-001). No request was ever served.

**Structure (Priya, Kavya):**
- A coherent thin-controller pattern across 91 controllers (principal, then `context.require*()`, then service, then `ApiResponse`).
- ShedLock on every scheduled job.
- An outbox email worker with SKIP LOCKED leases.
- Four crash-recovery reaper and sweep jobs.
- Fail-closed startup validators for secrets and AI URLs.

**Weaknesses:**
- 13 services are over 800 lines (`DealService` 2,523) and there are package cycles (`service` to `web.dto`, `security` to `service`) with no architecture enforcement (EV-106).
- `@Transactional` is inert on self-invoked Meera write paths (EV-037).
- About 12 `catch(DataIntegrityViolationException)` blocks around a plain `save()` are dead code (EV-047).
- 40+ `@Async` handlers share Spring's unbounded default executor with no uncaught-exception handler (EV-046).
- There are no outbound timeouts or circuit breakers on Meta, Shopify and Gemini (EV-045).
- Redis is never wired for the API in prod (EV-017).
- Spring Boot 3.3.5 is out of OSS support; the OSV scan found 32 advisories on the pinned Tomcat 10.1.31 (EV-054).

## 14. API

**Status: NOT READY.** **E3 / C82.**

**Surface:** 91 `@RestController` classes and 342-343 mappings under `/api/v1`. There is zero `@PreAuthorize`. Role separation is done at the filter chain (`/admin/**` gets ADMIN, `/creator/**` gets CREATOR) plus per-service ownership checks. Money endpoints consistently require `Idempotency-Key`. Escrow amounts are always derived on the server (Vikram-API).

**Defects:**
- **EV-005 (P0).** `POST /campaigns` accepts a client-settable `status=ACTIVE` and never calls `requireFundedEscrow` or `chargeOnPublish`. Only `update()` does (Tara confirmed this at `CampaignService.java:369,376`). Both real FE publish entry points post ACTIVE to `create()`.
- **EV-004 plus XR-PRIYA-01 (P0).** `InternalServiceTokenFilter` matches the raw `getRequestURI()`. So `/api/v1/%69nternal/meera/turns/release` passes the Spring firewall, skips the filter, and is still routed to `MeeraInternalController` (Kabir's runtime library probe). The same bug class was already fixed in `AuthRateLimitFilter`.
- **EV-064.** `PATCH /campaigns/{id}` lacks `@Valid`.
- **EV-079.** The on-behalf JWT `jti` is never enforced for replay.
- **EV-141.** Three notification endpoints return bare DTOs, not the `ApiResponse` envelope.
- **EV-149.** Report export is always served as `application/octet-stream`.
- **EV-111.** 46 of 91 controllers have no controller-level test, and `CampaignController` has none.

**Endpoints with no frontend caller (EV-041):**
- contract cancel and amend;
- escrow payout and refund (refund is documented as operator-only);
- external-creator delete;
- festival link-existing;
- custom-email cancel;
- creator-agent monthly cap;
- brand reanalyze;
- admin MFA reset;
- `GET /wallet/balance`.

**Positive:** The FE-to-BE contract for the main app is clean: 194 calls, 0 stale (EV-131).

## 15. Database

**Status: PARTIAL.** The schema applies, but money columns are wrong-sized and there are no backups. **E4 / C80.**

**Executed:**
- 136 migrations applied cleanly to a real MySQL 8 throwaway schema, which was then dropped (meera-run).
- Meera-DB's scripted diff of 87 entities against the replayed migration chain (89 tables) found 0 missing entity columns, no duplicate versions and no `BigDecimal` on a non-DECIMAL column.
- Meera-DB counted 140 migration files and meera-run reported 136 applied. The inputs do not reconcile these two numbers.

**Defects:**
- **Affiliate ENUMs (EV-011, runtime-confirmed).** The `wallet_transactions` `type` and `reference_type` ENUMs (V8) were never widened for `AFFILIATE_COMMISSION` and `AFFILIATE_EARNING`, so every affiliate ledger insert fails under strict mode.
- **Withdrawal keys (EV-014).** The withdrawal idempotency key is 80/82 characters against `VARCHAR(64)`.
- **Double-fund (EV-002).** There is no uniqueness on `escrow_holds.milestone_id`, and `PaymentMilestone.markFunded` overwrites unconditionally.
- **No actor attribution (XR-KABIR-01).** `escrow_holds` and `wallet_transactions` have no actor column.
- **Edited migrations (EV-083).** Three migrations were edited after first commit, a checksum risk.
- **Missing indexes (EV-132):** `password_reset_tokens.token_hash`, `coupon_codes.code`, `payouts.status`, `plans.razorpay_plan_id`.
- **Latent ENUM drift (EV-130).**
- **Out-of-order versioning.** Mixed versioning with `outOfOrder` enabled (EV-143).
- **Cascades and ids.** Invoice cascades and non-ULID ids in a data migration (EV-156).
- **Dead column.** `wallets.escrow_balance` is never written (EV-135).
- **Plaintext tax ids.** Creator GSTIN and PAN are stored in plaintext (EV-069).
- **No backup automation** anywhere in the repo for the ledger (EV-093). The CEO elevated this to a launch condition.

**Testing gap:** The `@DataJpaTest` suites use H2 create-drop and never execute Flyway, so ENUM and VARCHAR drift is invisible to `mvn test` (EV-038, RC-03). CI does run `flyway-validate.yml` against a real `mysql:8.0` on migration changes (q-answers).

## 16. Authentication

**Status: CONDITIONAL.** Core JWT is sound, but there is an admin MFA gap. **E3 / C78.**

**Positives (Kabir):**
- JWT HS256 with fail-closed secret validation.
- jjwt `verifyWith().parseSignedClaims()`, so no algorithm confusion.
- Refresh rotation, and an HttpOnly `SameSite=Strict` refresh cookie.
- BCrypt(12).
- Hashed OTPs with 3 attempts.
- Enumeration-safe forgot-password.
- Suspension re-evaluated on every refresh (blueprint-auth).

**Defects:**
- **Admin MFA re-enrolment (EV-022, P1).** The admin MFA secret can be re-enrolled with a bare JWT while MFA is already enabled. That allows persistent takeover, or lockout of the real admin.
- **Admin rate limit (EV-023).** There is no IP bucket on admin login or refresh. Downgraded to P2 because a per-account 5-attempt / 15-minute lockout exists.
- **Untested admin auth (EV-053).** `AdminAuthService` login, lockout, refresh and MFA-at-login have no tests.
- **Enumeration (EV-059).** Login discloses account existence and type before the password check.
- **Token survival (EV-071).** Account deletion needs no re-auth, and access tokens survive deletion, suspension and member removal for up to 15 minutes.
- **Refresh reuse (EV-129).** There is no refresh-token reuse detection.
- **Token purge (EV-127).** There is no purge job for revoked refresh tokens.
- **Suspension check (EV-140).** `resetPassword` and `changePassword` check only `users.status`, which is never written with SUSPENDED.
- **Dead matcher (EV-110).** There is a `permitAll` matcher for `/auth/verify-email` with no backing endpoint.
- **Operational risks (q-answers).** No `Domain` attribute is set on the refresh cookie, so a domain move would silently log everyone out. JWT secret rotation is a hard cutover with no dual-secret window.

**Refuted:** EV-034, "client IP collapses to the Docker gateway", was REFUTED. `forward-headers-strategy: native` is in place, and 3 of 3 regression tests executed.

## 17. Authorization

**Status: NOT READY.** Money actions are not role-gated. **E3 / C85.**

**Defects:**
- **Brand self-refund (EV-015, P0, upgraded).** `EscrowService.refund` checks only FUNDED plus OWNER/ADMIN plus "no active dispute". Because `ON_POSTED` is the only release condition ever assigned, a brand can claw back 100% of milestones after approval.
- **VIEWER approve (EV-021, P1).** `BrandDeliverableService.approve` uses `requireBrandWorkspace` only, so a VIEWER can approve. It is masked today only by EV-019. **Sequencing matters:** fix EV-021 before EV-019.
- **Monthly cap (EV-036, P1).** `setMonthlyCap` has no role-tier or MFA check.
- **Contracts and webhook secret (EV-070).** Any member can sign contracts as the brand and rotate the conversion-webhook secret.
- **Feature flag (EV-082).** 4 of 7 `CreatorAgentController` routes skip the feature-flag check.
- **No brand matcher (EV-099).** There is no `hasRole(BRAND)` matcher at the filter chain. The register rates this P2, while Kabir argues P1 because EV-021, EV-070 and EV-036 are three confirmed instances of exactly this gap (unresolved).
- **Workspace claim trust (Kabir IDOR sweep).** 70 methods authorize only through `requireBrandWorkspace` (the JWT workspace claim) without `requireMember`/`requireRole`. Money paths do re-check, except deliverable approve.
- **No actor on money moves (XR-KABIR-01).** Nothing records which member funded, released or refunded a hold.

**Positives:**
- `/admin/**` and `/creator/**` are filter-gated.
- There is no self-role-escalation path in `WorkspaceMemberController`.
- Every id-taking endpoint takes an `AuthPrincipal`, except the intentionally public ones.

## 18. Security

**Status: NOT READY.** **E3 / C80.**

**P0s:**
- **Committed keys (EV-006).** Live-format Anthropic and Sarvam keys are in `influora-ai/env.example:12,19`. R2 key id and secret and MSG91 keys are in `influora-api/env.example:50,51,67,71`. They also appear in git history: 5 commits for the ai file and 3 for the api file, per the register. The file header wrongly says it has no real secrets. Liveness was not tested. Rotation is a same-day action.
- **Internal-path bypass (EV-004 plus XR-PRIYA-01).** A logged-in brand already receives the on-behalf token, so it can refund every Meera turn credit or forge assistant messages.

**Further issues:**
- **Kavya's note.** `application.yml` carries committed non-blank defaults for R2 and MSG91 values. They are not in `SecretsStartupValidator`'s list, so prod would boot on them silently. Kavya flagged this to Kabir; it was not scored because the values were not inspected.
- **Tokens in URLs (EV-092).** Reset and invite tokens travel in query strings on pages that load GTM (GA4, Clarity, Meta Pixel).
- **Dependencies (EV-054, EV-078).** Out-of-support platform, plus pyjwt and python-multipart advisories.
- **Public API docs (EV-040).** influora-ai serves `/docs`, `/redoc` and `/openapi.json` publicly, and silently drops routers that fail to import while `/readyz` stays green.
- **Live edge headers (EV-010).** CSP/HSTS/XFO on the live edge are unverified. Downgraded to P2 at E2/C30: the committed image sets them, and live differs.
- **Unsubscribe (EV-116).** Unsubscribe mutates state on GET with non-expiring tokens.
- **Rate-limit memory (EV-114).** The rate-limit map is never evicted.

**Positives (Kabir, runtime and static):**
- Webhook HMAC verifiers for Razorpay, Shopify, WooCommerce and conversion webhooks are fail-closed and constant-time.
- The SSRF guard pins DNS and re-validates redirects.
- The CORS allow-list is explicit.
- Uploads are magic-byte sniffed, with ClamAV in prod (which fails closed).
- Parameterised queries only.
- Sort whitelist.
- Dev seed runner is `@Profile(dev)`.
- No hardcoded DB or admin passwords in migrations.
- No secret-like files in history (`.pem`, `id_rsa`, ACCESS-GUIDE).

## 19. Payment

**Status: NOT READY.** There are money-integrity P0s, and no money flow was exercised at runtime on this RC. **E3 / C85.**

**Money in (strongest path):**
- Razorpay order, HMAC webhook, row-locked idempotent credit with an amount/currency cross-check, double-entry ledger, pessimistic wallet locks, and `WalletTopUpReconciliationJob` hourly.
- Gaps: reconciliation gives up after 7 days and records the order id rather than the payment id (EV-145); there is no refund/chargeback event handling (EV-094); a stale subscription webhook can downgrade a re-subscribed workspace (EV-101).

**Escrow:**
- **Double-fund (EV-002, P0).** Reproduced in a scratch unit test (Meera-DB).
- **Brand self-refund (EV-015, P0).**
- **Unfunded publish (EV-005, P0).** Publish with no funded escrow and no fee.
- **Approval never releases (EV-019, runtime-confirmed).** `Deliverable.milestoneId` is never populated.
- **VIEWER release path (EV-021).**
- **Release gate off (EV-095).** The CR-51 release gate is off by default and fails open.
- **Pool hold (EV-084).** A campaign-level pool hold satisfies activation but has no release or refund path.
- **Deferred catch (EV-166).** A swallow after an inner `@Transactional` cannot protect the outer commit.

**Money out:**
- **Every withdrawal fails (EV-014).**
- **Gateway inside the transaction (EV-003, downgraded to P1, flag-gated off).** RazorpayX is called inside the open transaction while holding wallet and clearing-wallet locks with no timeout, and idempotency is marked COMPLETED before commit.
- **Payouts manual (EV-020).** `VITE_PAYOUTS_ENABLED` is UI-only, with no backend gate, and a placeholder RazorpayX config passes `isConfigured()`. Payouts are manual SUPER_ADMIN UTR entries.
- **Failure handling (EV-035, P2).** RazorpayX "failed" is not treated as failure, and every exception is treated as definitive.
- **Admin replay (EV-163).** Admin manual payout replay 500s.

**Affiliate (RC-16):**
- ENUMs rejected (EV-011).
- Wrong wallet owner id (EV-025).
- Funded from the platform clearing wallet with no brand debit, and uncapped (EV-033).
- Period-agnostic sweep (EV-161).
- Hardcoded flat 10% commission (EV-043).

**Fees and tax:**
- The platform fee resolves from four divergent sources (EV-060).
- The publish fee is charged on `budgetMax` and is non-refundable (EV-087).
- Gateway fees are never netted (EV-090).
- GST defaults to IGST on a blank GSTIN (EV-168).
- TDS only on the manual path (EV-031).

**Why the green tests do not settle this.** 162 money-path tests pass (Vikram-Pay), but they are Mockito-based and structurally cannot see column-length, DB-ENUM, owner-id or transaction-ordering defects.

## 20. AI

**Status: PARTIAL.** Cost controls are present; key flows are broken or unproven. **E3 / C70.**

**Executed:**
- pytest 874/875. The one failure is the SSE heartbeat timing flake (EV-112).
- 670 AI-related Java tests green.
- Offline evals exit 1: 5 datasets pass, and 2 have zero fixtures (EV-057).
- The n8n tagger sync check passes.

**Models (Ash):** `claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929` for Meera chat and brand-safety; `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001` for TrendSpark, trend tag and Copilot; `gemini-2.5-flash` for analyze-site and voice cleanup; Sarvam saarika:v2 for STT and bulbul:v3 for TTS. No tool schema uses `anyOf/oneOf/allOf`.

**Positive controls (static plus tests):**
- Money tools are excluded from the offered tool set and are scope-gated and role-gated.
- Replayed history cannot forge `tool_use`/`tool_result`.
- Untrusted text is delimiter-wrapped and neutralised.
- A truncated `tool_use` is never salvaged.
- Every provider-bound route passes the $15/day global spend gate, and there is a kill switch.
- The creator consent gate runs before any creator provider call.
- The Redis outage path degrades gracefully, with an under-count fix (q-answers).

**Defects:**
- **Meera funding dead (EV-024).**
- **Inert transactions (EV-037).** `@Transactional` is inert on multi-write Meera paths, so a retry can duplicate a campaign.
- **Launch without gates (EV-074).** `ConfirmLaunchExecutor` activates without `CampaignValidator` gates.
- **One workspace can exhaust the budget (EV-044).** There is no per-workspace blocking cap in prod and chat history is unbounded, so one workspace can use up the $15/day global ceiling.
- **Gemini timeouts (EV-045).** No timeouts on Gemini calls.
- **Brand-safety batch (EV-091).** GARM batches of 25 cannot fit `max_tokens=4096` or the 30-second timeouts. The feature is flag-off.
- **Niche matching (EV-076).** `show_creators` matches the niche against name and bio, not categories.
- **Regions (EV-139).** `APPROVED_LLM_REGIONS` is declared but never enforced.
- **Router drops (EV-040).** Router import failures are swallowed.
- **TrendSpark (EV-013).** Off everywhere, and also blocked by EV-001.
- **Copilot jobs off by default (EV-068).**
- **Credit meter UI never rendered (EV-065).**

**Model-quality claims.** No live provider call was made, so every model-quality property (rails adherence, GARM accuracy, latency) is **unmeasured**. No AI accuracy figure is claimed in this report.

## 21. Automation

**Status: NOT READY.** Key jobs are off or untested. **E3 / C70.**

**Mature:**
- 24 scheduled job units, all ShedLocked.
- A dedicated 10-thread TaskScheduler.
- An outbox pattern with claim, send and mark phases, plus exponential backoff.
- Two-layer webhook idempotency.
- Per-row try/catch in batch jobs.
- msa executed 257 job and supporting tests green.

**Defects:**
- **TrendPullJob (EV-013, runtime-observed).** It fails closed without `TREND_INGEST_CLASSIFIER_WORKSPACE_ID`, and no committed config sets it.
- **Untested money jobs (EV-048).** `IdempotencyReservationReaperJob`, `SubscriptionDunningJob`, `SubscriptionRenewalResetJob`, `AICreditResetJob`, `CreatorThemeTaggingJob` and the invoice-failure retry have no tests.
- **Unbounded async (EV-046).**
- **Copilot and nudge jobs off (EV-068, EV-150).** Neither has an operator metric.
- **Postiz (EV-107).** Exists only in skill docs; there is no code.
- **Orphaned n8n workflow (EV-113).** Inactive, a Google Trends stub, and not equivalent to the Java job. The system of record is unclear.
- **CR-51 gate off (EV-095).**
- **Email duplicate-send window (EV-115).** Self-documented, under degraded SMTP with multiple instances.
- **Bulk email (EV-050).** Returns 501.
- **Invoice-failure retries (q-answers).** They exhaust to `log.error` "requires manual backfill", with no admin UI reading `escrow_invoice_failures`.
- **Health check does not restart (q-answers).** Docker `restart: unless-stopped` does not act on an unhealthy HEALTHCHECK, and no watchdog exists.

## 22. Testing

**Status: NOT READY as release evidence.** **E4 / C85.**

| Suite | Result | Caveat |
|---|---|---|
| Java unit/repository (`mvn clean test`) | 3,621 run, 0 fail, 22 skipped | The 22 skipped are every full-context and real-MySQL test. There is no Docker-free context smoke test (XR-PRIYA-02), which is why EV-001 shipped. |
| Flyway on real MySQL 8 | 136 applied | Throwaway schema; not the prod `flyway_schema_history`. |
| Vitest | 1,348 pass, 2 fail, 3 skipped | EV-055 is deterministic and hides the F-0459 guard. |
| pytest (influora-ai) | 874/875 | EV-112 flake: 2 fails and 1 pass across 3 isolated reruns. |
| Offline AI evals | exit 1 | 2 datasets have 0 fixtures (EV-057). |
| eslint | 7 errors | Not run in CI (XR-KAVYA-01). |
| Playwright e2e | Cannot run | `@playwright/test` is not declared, and no CI runs it (EV-052). |
| Job tests | 215 job-package plus 42 supporting, green (msa) | 5 money/compliance jobs are untested (EV-048). |

**Systemic gaps:**
- Mocked tests passed on real DB defects (EV-011, EV-014, EV-025; root cause RC-03).
- 46 of 91 controllers have no HTTP-layer test (EV-111).
- There are no tests for: create() to ACTIVE, re-fund, refund-after-approval, VIEWER approve, `AdminAuthService` login/MFA (EV-053), invoicing and tax-identity (EV-056), shipments (EV-066), `PlatformFeeAdminService` (EV-030), `AdminCampaignService` (EV-077), or `BillingController` checkout (EV-073).
- An affiliate reconciliation test is intermittent under parallel runs (EV-121).
- Kavya's systemic point: no agent pulled real GitHub Actions run history, so every CI claim is inferred from YAML. EV-038's framing is disputed on exactly this point.

**Standing rule.** Execution proofs go to Kabir or Meera; they must not be passed "by analysis".

## 23. Performance

**Status: UNMEASURED.** **E2 / C55.** There is no load or latency test for the RC.

**Static and build evidence:**
- A single 3.37 MB (899 KB gzip) entry chunk. 92 routes are statically imported and there are only 4 non-route `lazy()` calls (EV-039).
- No outbound timeouts or breakers on Meta Graph, Meta OAuth, Shopify or Gemini (EV-045).
- N+1 queries in `PortfolioService`, reachable from the public unauthenticated portfolio page (EV-072; fixed only in the uncommitted F-0588).
- The M-17 facets cache is built but not wired, so creator search still scans 5,000 rows per request (EV-061).
- Hikari max pool (10 dev / 20 prod) is far narrower than Tomcat's thread count (EV-167).
- Row locks are held across a blocking RazorpayX call (EV-003).
- Admin endpoints load whole tables into memory (EV-151).
- `CreatorApplicationService.list()` loads a creator's full history before paginating (EV-160).
- influora-ai is capped at one uvicorn worker by design (EV-157).
- There is no `@EntityGraph` anywhere.
- The on-behalf JWT is verified twice per call: fix `92c81be` is not an ancestor of the RC (q-answers).

## 24. DevOps

**Status: NOT READY.** **E3 / C78.**

**Blockers and defects:**
- **Outage on deploy.** Deploying this RC causes a full backend outage (EV-001).
- **Ungated images (EV-063).** api and ai images publish to GHCR on every main push as `:latest` with no test gate. Only the web job has money-flag gates. The flag gate checks the RazorpayX placeholder, not the Razorpay key id (q-answers).
- **Prerender and redirects (EV-009, runtime-confirmed live).** The Docker build runs `npx vite build` directly and skips prerender and sitemap, and nginx never reads `_redirects`.
- **Redis unwired (EV-017).** Redis is not wired for the API in any prod compose, and CI uses Boot-2 env names that silently no-op.
- **No backups (EV-093).**
- **No observability (EV-088).** Health plus console logs only; no metrics, alerting or cross-service correlation.
- **Unreproducible live stack (EV-010).**
  - There are 4 committed compose topologies, and the live box runs an untracked compose file.
  - `api.influora.in` serves a certificate for another domain.
  - The CI-published web image bakes `https://api.influora.in/api/v1`, while `.env.production` uses the apex `influora.in/api/v1`.
- **Undefined next RC (EV-018).**

**From q-answers:**
- `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=REPLACE_ME` is left for manual fill in `generate-env.sh`.
- No memory limits or OOM handling in any compose.
- No Flyway startup timeout.
- No env-drift monitor for the Utho `influora.env`.

**Positives:**
- A Flyway validate workflow runs against real MySQL in CI.
- Fail-closed startup validators.
- ClamAV is fail-closed.

## 25. Code Quality

**Measurements (Kavya, Priya):**
- 834 main Java files and 564 TSX files.
- 13 Java services over 800 lines; 7 methods over 150 lines (`CreateCampaignExecutor.doExecute` is 276).
- 15 TSX files over 1,100 lines (`creator-chat` 3,272, `brand-chat` 3,003) (EV-106).
- Three FE API layers: `api.ts` 7,037 lines, `meera-api.ts` 905, admin `api-contracts.ts` 1,546. `POST /wallet/escrow/fund` is wrapped in two of them. A DTO drift guard covers 5 of about 403 DTOs (EV-108).
- 234 broad catch blocks, of which about 12 are dead (EV-047).
- 12 of 89 controllers return no `ApiResponse` envelope.
- 0 `console.log`, 0 `@ts-ignore`, and effectively no `any` in non-test source.

**Hygiene (EV-109):**
- Mock fixtures in the prod bundle.
- 1,676 tracked `.proof-os` files (22 MB).
- A scratch `.class`.
- Next.js leftovers with `ignoreBuildErrors`.
- Two lockfiles.
- A stale `styles/globals.css`.

**Stale or wrong comments (RC-14):** EV-027, EV-110, EV-124, EV-125, EV-126, EV-134, EV-148. Consistent with the project's standing observation that comments in this repo lie.

**Positive:** The code carries unusually detailed post-mortem commentary for real prior incidents: F-01/F-03/F-05 cost fixes, H-22, CR-22a, Kabir NEW-1. Reviewers note these read as genuine incident fixes rather than aspirational claims.

## 26. Anti-Vibe-Code Engineering Audit

**Question: does this codebase read as an engineered system, or as a collection of features?**

**Answer: Both. It is engineered at the component level and assembled at the system level.** Priya's verdict is "coherent code, incoherent deploy/config layer": "a well-engineered collection of features on a single-node architecture". Kavya's is "REJECTED for go-live in two areas, CONDITIONAL elsewhere". The evidence supports both.

**Engineered at the component level:**
- Individual services show deliberate, incident-driven engineering: ShedLock everywhere, an outbox with leases, pessimistic locks on money rows, server-derived escrow amounts, fail-closed validators, triple-gated AI money tools, and constant-time HMAC verifiers.

**Assembled at the system level:**
- Invariants that span components are not owned by any one component. They are re-implemented per caller and drift. The cause-by-cause table below shows where.

### 26.1 Cause-by-cause analysis

| Cause | What the evidence shows | Verdict | EV |
|---|---|---|---|
| Architecture | A clean 3-service split with JWKS/ES256 service tokens and HMAC internal calls. But state is per-process (OAuth state, rate limits, nonces, one uvicorn worker), so the system is single-instance only. The deploy layer is fragmented: 4 committed topologies plus an untracked live one. | Mixed | EV-062, EV-157, EV-010 |
| Patterns | A consistent thin-controller pattern (principal, `require*()`, service, `ApiResponse`) across 91 controllers. But authorization is left to each service, so it is applied inconsistently. | Engineered pattern, inconsistent application | RC-05: EV-021, EV-036, EV-070, EV-099 |
| Abstractions | Missing where they matter most. There is no single campaign-activation guard: rules are duplicated across `create()`, `update()`, resume and Meera `confirm_launch`. There is no milestone state guard on funding. There is no shared decoded-path utility for security filters. The fee is resolved from 4 sources. | Feature-by-feature | EV-005, EV-074, EV-080 (RC-04); EV-002 (RC-01); EV-004, XR-PRIYA-01; EV-060 |
| Tests | 3,621 green unit tests, but they are Mockito/H2 at the layer where the bugs live. There is no context-load smoke test, every real-MySQL test is Docker-gated, and 46 of 91 controllers have no HTTP test. | Volume without the right shape | RC-03, RC-15; XR-PRIYA-02, EV-038, EV-111 |
| Incompleteness | Built-but-not-wired: support tickets, KYC doc viewing, credit meter, contract cancel/amend, facets cache, Postiz, newsletter. | Collection-of-features symptom | RC-07: EV-012, EV-016, EV-041, EV-061, EV-065; EV-049, EV-107 |
| Hardcoding | `meera_demo_campaign` in a live money path, a mock theme on a live page, admin metrics hardcoded to 0, a flat 10% affiliate commission, and the FE hardcoding a 15% fee. | Placeholders presented as data | EV-024, EV-043, EV-060 |
| Separation | Package cycles (`service` to `web.dto`, `security` to `service`), no ArchUnit, god classes, and 3 FE API layers. | Weak | EV-106, EV-108 |
| Database | The schema tracks the entities exactly (0 missing columns). But DB constraints do not encode the business invariants: no one-active-hold-per-milestone rule, ENUMs are not widened with the Java enums, and key length is below the key format. There are also edited migrations and out-of-order versioning. | Structurally sound, invariant-poor | EV-002, EV-011, EV-014, EV-083, EV-143 |
| API | A clean contract with the main FE (0 stale calls), and money endpoints are idempotency-gated. But the create path skips gates, `@Valid` is missing, and there are 9 or more orphan endpoints. | Mostly engineered | EV-131, EV-005, EV-064, EV-041 |
| Errors | Dead `DataIntegrityViolation` catches, a swallow after an inner transaction, invoice and GST failures that only log, an unbounded async executor with no handler, and gateway exceptions treated as definitive. | Inconsistent | EV-047, EV-166, EV-153, EV-046, EV-035 |
| Security | Strong primitives: HMAC, SSRF guard, CORS, jjwt, BCrypt. But a known bug class was fixed in one filter and not its sibling, and keys were committed in example files. | Strong primitives, no cross-cutting enforcement | EV-004, XR-PRIYA-01, EV-006 |
| AI | Well-engineered safety architecture: gated money tools, neutralised untrusted text, a spend gate on every route. But there is no eval evidence, transactions are inert on the write paths, and there is no per-workspace cap. | Engineered with gaps | EV-057, EV-037, EV-044 |
| Deployment | Not reproducible from the repo: untracked live compose, `:latest` ungated images, Redis unwired, prerender skipped, no backups or metrics, and a boot-breaking bean shipped. | Collection of deploys | RC-06: EV-009, EV-010, EV-017, EV-063, EV-088, EV-093; EV-001 |
| Debt | Heavy but visible: stale javadoc, repo hygiene, 1,676 tracked `.proof-os` files, and out-of-support dependencies. | Managed but growing | RC-14, EV-109, EV-054 |
| Properly engineered areas | Money-in (top-up webhook plus reconciliation), deal lifecycle and messaging (idempotent, row-locked, SSE reconnect), contracts (locked signing), deliverable upload (MIME sniffing, strict URL validation), team invites (E5/C92), email outbox, client error reporting, JWT, webhook verifiers, AI prompt-injection hygiene, and fabrication-free marketing proof points. | Engineered | Blueprints (deals, team, notifications), Kabir positives, EV-120, EV-131 |

### 26.2 Proper Production Engineering Blueprint

Current state, the evidence for it, and the recommended target, in priority order 1 to 10. **No rewrite is recommended.** The evidence points to missing invariants and missing enforcement around sound components, not to unsound components.

| Priority | Theme | Current | Evidence | Recommended |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Critical risks | The backend cannot boot. Keys are in git. A regulated custody claim is false on the live site. | EV-001, EV-006, EV-007 | `@Autowired` on the `TrendPullJob` constructor, plus a Docker-free `@SpringBootTest` context smoke test in every `mvn test` (XR-PRIYA-02). Rotate keys, use placeholders, purge history. Rewrite the custody, "never pools", RBI-PA, "guaranteed" and TDS copy. |
| 2 | Security | Raw-URI internal gate; MFA re-enrol with a bare JWT; tokens in URLs; out-of-support Spring Boot. | EV-004, XR-PRIYA-01, EV-022, EV-092, EV-054 | A shared `decode(stripContext(uri))` utility for every path-matching filter, plus an architecture test that bans raw `getRequestURI()` in `security/`. Require the current TOTP to re-enrol. Move tokens to POST bodies or fragments. Upgrade to Spring Boot 3.5.x. |
| 3 | Data integrity | DB constraints lag the code. | EV-011, EV-014, EV-002, XR-KABIR-01, EV-083, EV-093 | New migrations: widen the ENUMs; widen or hash the idempotency keys; add a unique active hold per milestone; add `actor_user_id` on escrow and ledger rows. Never edit applied migrations, and diff a fresh schema against prod in CI. Restore-tested nightly off-box backups. |
| 4 | Payment integrity | State and role invariants are enforced by callers, not by the domain. | EV-002, EV-015, EV-005, EV-021, EV-019, EV-003, EV-033 | A milestone and escrow state machine that owns fund, refund and release preconditions. One `CampaignActivationGuard` for every ACTIVE transition (F-0848 exists uncommitted). An OWNER/ADMIN gate on approve, then link deliverables to milestones. Gateway calls outside the DB transaction, with a durable Payout row first. A brand-funded affiliate path. A server-side PAYOUTS flag plus placeholder detection. |
| 5 | Reliability | No timeouts or breakers; unbounded async; Redis unwired; single instance; no alerting. | EV-045, EV-046, EV-017, EV-062, EV-088 | Connect and read timeouts on all outbound clients. A bounded named executor with an uncaught handler and a DLQ table. Wire `SPRING_DATA_REDIS_HOST` plus a `CacheErrorHandler` fallback. Metrics and alerts for 5xx, reconciliation repairs, payout orphans and clearing-wallet balance. |
| 6 | Correctness | Built-but-unwired features; placeholders shown as data; provenance not filtered. | EV-012, EV-016, EV-024, EV-043, EV-008, EV-060 | Wire the support UI and KYC doc viewing. Remove the demo id and mocks. One fee source of truth. Enforce the `dataSource` filter on every "verified" read (post-RC fixes, to be re-verified). |
| 7 | Maintainability | God classes, cycles, 3 API clients, stale comments, no lint in CI. | EV-106, EV-108, RC-14, XR-KAVYA-01 | An ArchUnit freeze on current cycles. Split `DealService`, `CampaignService` and the chat pages along existing seams. One FE client with OpenAPI-generated types. A lint step in `frontend-checks.yml`. |
| 8 | Performance | One 3.37 MB chunk; N+1 on a public page; unwired cache; narrow pool. | EV-039, EV-072, EV-061, EV-167 | Route-level `React.lazy`. Batch the portfolio queries (F-0588). Wire the facets cache. Size Hikari against measured concurrency. A baseline load test. |
| 9 | Scalability | Per-process state; one uvicorn worker; whole-table admin reads. | EV-062, EV-157, EV-151 | Redis-backed rate limits, nonces and OAuth state, so 2 or more API replicas are possible. Paginate admin aggregates. Revisit the AI worker cap once the spend tracker is Redis-authoritative. |
| 10 | Nice-to-have | SEO prerender, structured data, dark-mode toggle, dead components. | EV-009, EV-158, EV-172, EV-133, EV-137 | Run the postbuild in the Dockerfile and translate `_redirects` to nginx 301s. Add FAQ JSON-LD. Delete dead code. |

## 27. Product

**Status: NOT SELF-SERVE END-TO-END.** **E3 / C82** (Swapnil).

**Brand journey as coded:**
1. Self-serve email-OTP signup.
2. KYC submitted, which leaves the brand PENDING.
3. **Manual admin KYC approval.** The admin cannot see the uploaded GST/PAN documents and is never notified (EV-016).
4. Razorpay wallet top-up (self-serve).
5. Publish. 10% of `budgetMax` is debited (7% on Pro), and the fee is non-refundable (EV-087).
6. Discover and invite creators, or creators apply. External creators need a manual admin import (EV-075).
7. Negotiate the deal; generate the contract and both parties e-sign.
8. Fund the milestone escrow from the wallet.
9. The creator submits.
10. The brand approves, and **no money moves** (EV-019).
11. The brand clicks a separate release. The 15% commission is deducted and the net is credited to the creator's wallet.

**Creator journey as coded:**
1. Signup and onboarding.
2. Apply or accept an invite, then deliver.
3. Wait for the brand to release.
4. **Withdraw is disabled** in the production build. A SUPER_ADMIN pays manually outside the app and records a bank UTR (EV-020).

Creator identity KYC is self-declared, and PENDING is enough to pass (EV-058).

**Other product gaps:**
- There is no user support channel (EV-012).
- Brand wallet money can never leave the platform (EV-086).
- The marketplace starts empty: seed creators were deleted, and supply is organic signup plus manual import (EV-075).
- There is no in-product prompt toward the manual release step.

**Coded retention hooks (Swapnil):** notifications, the connect-nudge job (off by default), Meera and Copilot (Copilot off by default), Meta-synced analytics, reviews, portfolio pages, HYPE campaigns, and coupon and affiliate earnings (affiliate is broken, RC-16).

## 28. Business

Three categories, strictly separated. No customer counts, revenue, traction or market size appear in the inputs, and none are reported here.

**CODE-VERIFIED FACT.** These are true of RC code (E3, static). None was proven at runtime on this RC.
- Revenue mechanics in code:
  - a 10% brand publish fee on `budgetMax`, charged at publish (7% on Pro);
  - a 15% creator commission deducted at release;
  - a Pro plan at Rs 4,999/month (the 499,900-paise seed and FE copies are consistent, per Rohan's spot check, EV-100);
  - AI credit plans.
- Payment custody is an internal pooled ledger: brand wallet, then Influora's platform clearing wallet, then the creator wallet. There is no Razorpay Route or nodal account configuration (EV-007).
- One Razorpay account and one VPS are shared with another product, Snapsby (EV-144).
- The platform absorbs Razorpay fees by CEO ruling, but fees are never modelled or netted (EV-090).
- There is a global $15/day AI spend ceiling, a kill switch, and a creator cap of $0.75/month (Ash, Rohan).
- Payout operations are manual (EV-020). KYC review is manual (EV-016).

**BUSINESS ASSUMPTION.** Not provable from code; each needs a controlled test.
- Brands accept a combined take of roughly 25% (10% non-refundable fee on max budget plus the creator's 15%) (EV-087).
- Ops can staff same-day KYC review and manual payouts at launch volume.
- Creators accept that payment depends on the brand clicking release.
- A stored brand wallet with no exit is acceptable without a PA licence. A legal opinion is required.
- Meta-synced analytics differentiate enough to retain brands.

**FUTURE POSSIBILITY.** The code exists but is gated or unproven.
- RazorpayX self-serve payouts (backend exists, flag off, config unvalidated; also blocked by EV-014 and EV-003).
- The `ON_POSTED` release gate with Meta post verification (needs the cutover env plus Instagram Login approval).
- Meera autonomous campaign launch.
- Affiliate settlement credits (broken, RC-16).
- Pro plan auto-billing.
- A dedicated Razorpay entity and account before scale or any PA-licence application (EV-144, severity disputed).
- The PA licence itself is deferred by standing decision, so the copy must match (F-0851).

**Status: NOT READY for acquisition spend.** **E3 / C78.**

Tejas found that copy discipline is high in places. `PROOF_POINTS` documents the removal of fabricated traction statistics (8,915 creators, Rs 4.3 Cr paid, 24h payout) for ASCI and consumer-law reasons. Several pages carry inline banned-phrase lists. But the most load-bearing trust claims are false.

**Additional marketing risk:**
- Reset and invite tokens reach GTM-loaded pages (EV-092).
- Neha saw a "Duplicate Pixel ID" Meta Pixel console warning on every live page. It comes from the externally managed GTM container, not from repo code.

## 30. SEO

**Status: DEGRADED. Not a launch blocker.** **E5 / C90.**

**Runtime-confirmed on the live site, 2026-09-19 (EV-009, P2 after downgrade from P1):**
- The Docker build skips `postbuild` (`scripts/prerender.mjs`, `scripts/generate-sitemap.mjs`).
- nginx's `try_files ... /index.html` serves the same HTML for everything.
- `/sitemap.xml` returns 200 `text/html`.
- `/features/escrow` returns 200, not 301 (the `_redirects` file is Netlify syntax and is dead on nginx).
- No route has prerendered HTML.
- The private zones (`/brand/*`, `/admin/*`, `/creator/:handle`) are **not** noindexed in production, despite code comments saying they are. The result is duplicate content and soft-404s at site scale.

**Also:**
- Single-segment URLs soft-404 (EV-042).
- There is no FAQPage or Product JSON-LD despite stable Q&A content (EV-158).
- The 3.37 MB entry chunk is a Core Web Vitals risk (EV-039).

**Designed well on paper (Aditya):** per-route `<Seo>` tags, JSON-LD, `robots.txt` and `llms.txt`.

**Conflict:** Nisha rated sitemap and prerender VERIFIED WORKING, reading the source design. Aditya's live fetch shows the prerender step is BROKEN. The runtime evidence wins.

No Search Console, ranking or traffic data was available, and none is claimed.

**Questions that decided the outcome.** Each answer is condensed from the asking agent's file.

| Question (asker) | Answer | EV |
|---|---|---|
| Does the RC actually boot, and would any committed test have caught the TrendPullJob wiring defect? (xr-kavya) | It fails to boot. No: all 17 `TrendPullJobTest` cases use the non-Spring 7-arg constructor, and every full-context test is Docker-gated. | EV-001, XR-PRIYA-02 |
| Can a brand publish a campaign live without a funded escrow hold? (blueprint-campaigns) | Yes. `create()` never calls `requireFundedEscrow()`; only `update()` does. | EV-005 |
| Can a brand double-fund the same milestone through the real escrow API? (xr-kabir) | Yes. `assertContractActiveForMilestone` never checks `milestone.getStatus()`, and `markFunded` overwrites unconditionally. | EV-002 |
| Does `Deliverable.milestoneId` ever get populated? (skeptic EV-019..021) | No. The only builder call, in `ContractService.materializeDeliverables`, never sets it. | EV-019 |
| Can a non-service caller reach `/internal/meera/*`? (kabir) | Yes, via a percent-encoded path, proven with the exact Spring jars. | EV-004 |
| Can a brand go from signup to an ACTIVE campaign with no one on our team doing anything? (swapnil) | No. Admin KYC approval is manual, and the admin cannot view the documents. | EV-016 |
| Can a brand fund and launch through `/brand/meera` in live mode? (kavya) | No. There is a demo campaign id and a link to a non-existent route. | EV-024 |
| Do brand funds sit with a licensed PA in a segregated or nodal account? (tejas) | No. They sit in an internal pooled wallet ledger, and "nodal" appears nowhere in the backend. | EV-007 |
| Does the production build run prerender and sitemap? (aditya) | No. The Dockerfile runs `vite build` directly. Confirmed live. | EV-009 |
| Can the production stack be reproduced from the RC alone? (priya) | No. The live compose file is outside the repo, and the live SPA is a hand-built bundle. | EV-010 |
| Is there an enforced global daily AI spend ceiling? (rohan) | Yes, $15/day, called before every provider call. But there is no per-workspace cap in prod. | EV-044 |
| Can a forged or replayed Razorpay webhook credit a wallet? (vikram-pay) | Not with correct config: HMAC-SHA256, constant-time, fail-closed on a blank secret. | - (positive) |
| Does every `@Entity` column exist in the Flyway schema? (meera-db) | Yes. 0 missing columns across 87 entities. | - (positive) |
| Does the RC still contain the client-IP collapse bug? (skeptic EV-034) | No. It was fixed via `forward-headers-strategy: native`, and 3 of 3 regression tests were executed. | EV-034 (refuted) |
| Is escrow funding a Razorpay payment or a wallet debit? (blueprint-wallet) | Wallet balance only. `initiateFund` is synchronous against the wallet. | - |
| Does anything reach `SupportController` from a real brand or creator screen? (blueprint-notifications) | No. It is backend-only. | EV-012 |

## 33. Team Answers

**Pipeline.** The q-pipeline stage (the q-pipeline, q-tools and q-answerer skills) routed cross-lane and still-open questions to an answerer. Its only artifact in the inputs is `q-answers.json`: 30 answers, each with an evidence score, confidence and remaining uncertainty. The inputs do not record how the 30 questions were selected from the 473. The answerer notes that graphify was unavailable (no `graph.json` in the RC), so every answer came from direct Grep and Read of the clean RC (0 local changes). The per-agent inline answers (section 32) complement these.

**Premises the answerer found false, which are useful corrections in themselves:**
- There is no ECR. Images go to GHCR with the built-in `GITHUB_TOKEN`.
- There is no `MeeraPhaseB0BootValidationTest`. There is no `/creator/briefs` endpoint.
- `.cursor/rules` does not mirror `agents/*.md`.
- There is only one `vitest.gates.config.ts`.
- `TRUSTED_PROXIES` is inert. Tomcat `RemoteIpValve` replaced it.
- `MetaTokenRefreshService` is a synchronous `@Scheduled` job, not `@Async`.

**Answers that add new facts to the register:**
- Fix `92c81be` (single on-behalf JWT verify) is not an ancestor of the RC, so the double verification is live.
- `api.influora.in` is the push-default build arg while `.env.production` uses the apex domain.
- The money-flag gate checks only the RazorpayX placeholder, not `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID`.
- An unhealthy container is not restarted.
- There is no memory limit or OOM handling.
- There is no JWT secret-rotation path.
- The refresh cookie has no `Domain` attribute.
- `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=REPLACE_ME` is left for manual fill.
- Exhausted invoice failures have no admin UI.

## 34. Cross-Team Discussion

### 34.1 Agreements (independently re-confirmed by a cross-reviewer)

| Finding | Confirmed by | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| EV-001 boot failure | Priya, Kavya, verifier (runtime) | `TrendPullJob.java:156/175`; the 17 unit tests use the non-Spring constructor |
| EV-002 double-fund | Priya, Kabir, Meera-DB (scratch repro) | `EscrowService.java:388-434` has no status check; `markFunded` is unconditional |
| EV-003 gateway call inside the transaction | Priya, Kabir | Structural; runtime never reproduced |
| EV-004 internal bypass | Priya, Kabir (own runtime probe) | `InternalServiceTokenFilter.java:82-83` |
| EV-005 create() to ACTIVE | Priya, Kabir | `create()` at `:145-235` has no gates; both FE publish entries post ACTIVE |
| EV-006 committed keys | Kabir | Git history shape and entropy analysis |
| EV-007 custody claim false | Rohan and Tejas (second independent pass) | terms:24; escrow policy:5,13,59,63; `application.yml:401-406` |
| EV-011, EV-014 schema overflow and ENUM | Priya, Kabir | V8 ENUM; 80 characters against `VARCHAR(64)`; no `sql_mode` relaxation |
| EV-015 brand self-refund | Kabir, Rohan and Tejas | `refundInternal` checks only dispute block and FUNDED |
| EV-017 Redis unwired | Priya ("more strongly than stated": zero `spring.data.redis.*` keys) | `application.yml` |
| EV-021, EV-025, EV-036 | Kabir | Direct reads |
| EV-037, EV-048, EV-052, EV-053, EV-055, EV-056, EV-057, EV-111, EV-112, EV-121, EV-142 | Kavya | Direct reads and file searches |
| EV-093, EV-105, EV-085, EV-031 | Rohan and Tejas | Configuration search, graphify failure reproduced |
| Positives (not in the register) | Kabir | Webhook HMACs, SSRF guard, CORS, jjwt, no self-role escalation, no hardcoded DB passwords |

### 34.2 Disagreements (each side's evidence recorded; none averaged)

| Topic | Position A (evidence) | Position B (evidence) | Resolution in the decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| EV-003 evidence score | Verifier: E4/C75. The full transaction shape was re-traced, and the `IdempotencyService` javadoc confirms `markCompleted` commits in `REQUIRES_NEW`. | Priya: E3/C80. Static only; the trigger (commit failure after gateway accept) was never reproduced, and the rubric reserves high scores for code plus runtime. | P1 used; evidence contested; Testcontainers repro required |
| EV-015 severity | Verifier: UPGRADED to P0. `ON_POSTED` is the only release condition ever assigned, so this affects 100% of milestones. | Kabir, Rohan and Tejas: P1 in their agreement lists | P0 used |

## 36. P0 Findings

Ten P0s were opened as of the 2026-09-19 remediation pass: the original 9 (8 register entries plus XR-PRIYA-01) plus the newly-registered EV-175. They are ordered by the must-fix sequence. **As of commit `fb6d267` (2026-09-19), EV-001 and EV-175 are CLOSED. As of commit `de249ed` (2026-09-19), EV-002 is CLOSED. As of four further commits on 2026-09-20 (`be76665`, `9bb8055`, `6228d61`, `51577f5`, `c6ed523`, `a76ea77` — six commits in total), EV-004, EV-005, EV-006 (code part), EV-007 (copy part), EV-008 and EV-015 are all CLOSED — FIXED IN COMMITTED CODE, VERIFIED FROM THE COMMIT.** **Only 1 P0 remains open: XR-PRIYA-01** (not in the 2026-09-20 close list; see its own entry below). The money P0s (EV-002, EV-015, EV-005) are ordered together because they share root cause RC-01/RC-04; all three are now closed.

### EV-001 - influora-api at RC 115f698 cannot boot in any Spring profile: BeanCreationException on TrendPullJob (two constructors, no @Autowired)

- **Finding:** influora-api at RC 115f698 cannot boot in any Spring profile: BeanCreationException on TrendPullJob (two constructors, no @Autowired)
- **Severity:** P0
- **Evidence Score:** E5
- **Confidence:** 98 (register 95)
- **Status:** **CLOSED — FIXED IN COMMITTED CODE fb6d267, VERIFIED FROM THE COMMIT (suite + real boot + probes).** On the audited RC 115f698 itself, still VERIFIED as originally reproduced; verification verdict CONFIRMED-RUNTIME / provenance COMMITTED (the RC itself was never fixed — a later commit on the same branch was). The fix (2026-09-19) was first built and boot-verified in an uncommitted worktree, then committed as `fb6d267` and independently re-verified from that commit — see the third Remediation log entry above. New evidence score/confidence: E5 / 92.
- **2026-09-19 remediation:** `TrendPullJob.java`'s public constructor was annotated `@Autowired` (Vikram), reviewed and approved with notes by Kavya (QA Lead) and Priya (CTO), and backed by a new, non-vacuous `TrendPullJobWiringTest` (falsified and restored, sha256-verified, `mvn clean test` green 3,679/3,679 with 22 skipped). Meera independently ran a real `spring-boot:run` boot both with and without the fix: without it, boot fails exactly as this entry describes; with it, `TrendPullJob` resolves cleanly and the failure moves one bean deeper, to `newsApiTopHeadlinesClient` — the same defect class in 3 sibling `TrendSourceClient` beans, now tracked as **EV-175 (P0)**. This work initially existed only in the worktree `C:/Users/Sage world/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/fix-ev001` (detached on top of commit `f076c0f`).
- **2026-09-19 further update:** EV-175's identical `@Autowired` fix was then applied to all 3 sibling classes in the same worktree, reviewed and approved (APPROVE) by both Kavya and Priya (see EV-175's own remediation note and the Remediation log above), and a real `spring-boot:run` boot with both fixes applied **reached "Started InfluoraApiApplication"** on 3 separate runs, with Flyway applying 137 migrations and no `BeanCreationException` anywhere in the logs, and correctly served `/api/v1/health` (200 UP once Mail/Redis indicators were disabled), `/api/v1/config/public` (200), and auth-gated `/api/v1/campaigns`/`/api/v1/wallet` (401 as expected). This remained confined to the uncommitted worktree at the time.
- **2026-09-19 final remediation — CLOSED:** Both fixes were then committed to `feat/meera-creator-phase-e` as commit `fb6d267` (parent `7846d93`, **not pushed**). Kavya independently re-reviewed the committed diff (exactly the 6 expected files, 168 insertions/0 deletions) and APPROVEd it (no notes). Meera's commit-level verify pass, run from a **clean checkout of `fb6d267` itself** (not the worktree): `mvn -B clean test` (JDK 21.0.9) — BUILD SUCCESS, 3,730 run / 0 failures / 0 errors / 22 skipped, surefire XML agrees; a real `spring-boot:run` boot reached `Started InfluoraApiApplication in 49.231 seconds` with 0 matches for `BeanCreationException`/"No default constructor found" anywhere in the boot log; `/api/v1/health` → 200 UP, `/api/v1/config/public` → 200, `/api/v1/campaigns` and `/api/v1/wallet` (no token) → 401. Environment-only notes (not code defects): a short `TMP`/`TEMP` path was again needed for the local Tomcat/JDK 21 socket on this PC; `/api/v1/health` reports DOWN only when SMTP/Redis are absent and their health indicators are left on (pre-existing); `/api/v1/actuator/health` returns 401 (pre-existing, `SecurityConfig.java:85`). This finding is now **CLOSED**. It remains listed here (not deleted) because `fb6d267` is committed locally only — not pushed, not deployed, and no new RC has been cut from it — and because the other open P0s still make the overall decision NO-GO.
- **Evidence:** [meera-run:MRUN-01] Same, with SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE=dev (the documented way to relax that validator). Company-tax and Instagram-lookup validators now correctly downgrade to WARN. Flyway validated 136 migrations against the same throwaway DB, Hibernate/JPA context initialized, security filter chain built (jwtAuthenticationFilter, correlationIdFilter, planGateFilter, authRateLimitFilter, internalServiceTokenFilter all registered) -- BUT at singleton pre-instantiation: BeanCreationException creating bean 'trendPullJob' -> BeanInstantiationException: 'Failed to instantiate [com.influora.job.TrendPullJob]: No default constructor found' -> NoSuchMethodException: com.influora.job.TrendPullJob.<init>(). Application context never started; Tomcat never actually served a request. BUILD FAILURE, exit code 1, total time 01:30 min. | Arjun code re-check at RC: TrendPullJob declares a public 5-arg constructor delegating
- **Adversarial verification:** TrendPullJob (RC file, unconditional @Component, no profile/conditional gating, package com.influora.job scanned by default via @SpringBootApplication) declares two constructors at exact lines 156 (public, 5-arg) and 175 (package-private, 7-arg test seam), neither @Autowired -- matches the finder's citation exactly. Built the RC's own classpath (mvn -o dependency:build-classpath against its pinned Spring Boot 3.3.5/spring-beans 6.1.x) and compiled the RC's own TrendPullJob.class, then ran a standalone probe against a real Spring DefaultListableBeanFactory with a real AutowiredAnnotationBeanPostProcessor: getBean('trendPullJob') threw BeanCreationException -> BeanInstantiationException -> NoSuchMethodException, message 'No default constructor found', reproducing the exact claimed failure at runtime against the RC's real bytecode and exact Spring version. This is an unconditional context-refresh failure -- no profile, env var, or specific request path is required; the whole backend cannot boot. New evidence: Runtime-reproduced via a standalone JVM probe using the RC's own compiled class and its pinned spring-beans jar: DefaultListableBeanFactory.getBean('trendPullJob') threw BeanCreationException with message 'No default constructor found', an exact match to the claim.
- **Location:** influora-api/src/main/java/com/influora/job/TrendPullJob.java:156 (public 5-arg ctor), :175 (package-private 7-arg test-seam ctor)
- **Reproduction:** SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE=dev mvn spring-boot:run against a migrated MySQL -> BeanInstantiationException: No default constructor found for com.influora.job.TrendPullJob (reproduced twice, default and dev profiles).
- **Impact:** The API process never reaches Started; Tomcat never serves a request. Any deploy of this RC takes the entire backend down (all brand, creator, admin, payment, webhook and Meera traffic). Not caught because the only full-context tests are Testcontainers-gated and were skipped (no Docker).
- **Affected Features:** Entire influora-api backend, TrendSpark trend ingestion
- **Agents:** meera-run (finder models: sonnet); merged from meera-run:MRUN-01
- **Recommended Fix:** Annotate the public TrendPullJob constructor with @Autowired (or make the test seam a static factory); add a non-Docker @SpringBootTest context-load smoke test (H2) to CI so a bean-wiring failure fails the build.
- **Verification Required:** mvn spring-boot:run (dev profile) reaches "Started InfluoraApiApplication"; curl /api/v1/health returns 200; context-load test red on the old code, green on the fix.
- **Finder disagreement noted at merge:** msa rates scheduled_job TrendPullJob WORKING E4 (unit tests only); dev rates it NOT IMPLEMENTED (disabled by default). Neither booted the context. Runtime boot (meera-run) plus Arjun code re-check override both.

### EV-175 - Even with the EV-001 fix applied, influora-api still cannot boot: 3 sibling TrendSourceClient beans share the identical constructor-wiring defect

- **Finding:** With the EV-001 fix applied in the fix worktree, `spring-boot:run` (dev profile, real throwaway MySQL, no Docker) no longer fails on `trendPullJob`; it now fails on `newsApiTopHeadlinesClient` with the identical error: `BeanCreationException: Error creating bean with name 'newsApiTopHeadlinesClient' ... Failed to instantiate [com.influora.service.trendspark.ingest.NewsApiTopHeadlinesClient]: No default constructor found (NoSuchMethodException: NewsApiTopHeadlinesClient.<init>())`. `TmdbUpcomingClient` and `YouTubeMostPopularClient` carry the same unconditional-`@Component`-plus-two-untagged-constructors shape (public 1-arg production ctor, package-private 2-arg test-seam ctor, neither `@Autowired`) and, per Spring's fail-fast context refresh, would each throw the same error once the beans ahead of them resolve. `MetaOAuthService.java:81-82` was checked and correctly excluded — it already carries `@Autowired`.

- **2026-09-19 final remediation — CLOSED:** Both this fix and EV-001's were committed together to `feat/meera-creator-phase-e` as commit `fb6d267` (parent `7846d93`, **not pushed**). Kavya re-reviewed the committed diff and confirmed `@Autowired` sits on the public production constructor only in all 3 sibling classes (test seams untouched), that both new tests use `AnnotationConfigApplicationContext.register(Class)`, and that a mutation removing `@Autowired` reproduces the exact claimed `BeanCreationException` — **APPROVE**. Meera's commit-level verify pass, from a clean checkout of `fb6d267` itself: `mvn -B clean test` — 3,730 run / 0 failures / 0 errors / 22 skipped, including `TrendSourceClientsWiringTest` (3/3); a real `spring-boot:run` boot reached `Started InfluoraApiApplication in 49.231 seconds` with 0 matches for `BeanCreationException`/"No default constructor found"; `/api/v1/health` → 200 UP, `/api/v1/config/public` → 200, `/api/v1/campaigns` and `/api/v1/wallet` → 401. - **Evidence:** Producer (Vikram)'s same-class-scan (regex over 344 `@Component`/`@Service`/`@Repository`/`@Controller`/`@RestController`/`@Configuration` classes, hand-checked) found the same 3 hits. Reviewer Kavya independently re-derived the same 3 hits by direct file reads and confirmed a throwaway parameterized probe test (`ZzEv001SiblingProbeTest`, deleted afterward) failed all 3 with `errors=3`, each "No default constructor found," then confirmed the worktree was left clean (only the 2 EV-001 files touched). Reviewer Priya independently re-read all 3 files line-by-line and confirmed the identical shape, and separately confirmed `MetaOAuthService.java` is a correctly-excluded false positive. Meera's verify pass then proved it at runtime: baseline (`git stash` to the pre-fix state) boot fails on `trendPullJob`; with the fix applied, boot fails on `newsApiTopHeadlinesClient` instead — the exact next bean in `TrendPullJob`'s `List<TrendSourceClient>` dependency, confirming this is a real, currently-reachable sequential blocker and not a hypothetical one.

The next RC would still fail context refresh and still take down every brand, creator, admin, payment, webhook and Meera path, for the same underlying reason (constructor ambiguity invisible to unit tests that call constructors with `new`, per RC-03/XR-PRIYA-02).

Then re-run a real boot attempt (`spring-boot:run` or a full `@SpringBootTest`) rather than relying on isolated constructor-resolution unit tests, since neither this fix nor EV-001's has yet produced a boot that reaches "Started." **Done (2026-09-19, committed as `fb6d267`, verified from the commit):** applied exactly this way, and the resulting boot did reach "Started" — first in the worktree three times, then again from a clean checkout of the commit itself — see the remediation bullets above.
